PRESIDENT'S SECRETARIAT 
New Delhi, the 26th January 2026 


No. 40-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Bihar Police :— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
Kundan Krishnan, IPS Superintendent of Police GM 

2; Arjun Lal Sub-Inspector GM 

3. Jitendra Singh Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 27.03.2002 led by Sanjay Rai, Vakil Rai, Shashi Bhushan Singh and Jata Shanker Singh, all inmates of Chapra 
jail along with their supporters, infuriated by the orders of the Home Department, Bihar for their transfer from Chapra jail to 
Bhagalpur and Buxar jails, started scuffles with the jail employees, police personnel and magistrates, deputed to execute the 
above order of transfer. They damaged the gates of their wards and destroyed government property in the jail compound. Their 
violent actions badly disturbed the routine functioning of the jail and created disorder, panic and terror among other jail inmates 
and jail personnel. All the efforts made by the police, magistrates and jail authorities to pacify them went in vain. The 
government personnel were abused and forcibly driven out of the jail premises. These aggressive prisoners took complete 
control of the jail in their hands. This situation prevailed till 29.3.2002. During these two days, the mutinous prisoners had 
collected arms and explosives/ bombs from outside as they had total control of the jail campus and the road around the jail 
compound was a general thoroughfare from where their supporters managed to throw into the compound the above items. It 
may be mentioned that at this time there were approximately 1200 prisoners inside the Chapra Divisional Jail. 


On 30.03.2002, the situation worsened in order to salvage the situation, under the leadership of Kundan Krishnan, SP 
Saran who deployed police personnel, action was started to bring the jail campus back under administrative control from the 
rioting prisoners. Despite repeated warning, the rioting prisoners continued to use stones against the police party. Considering 
the gravity of situation and having no alternative, Kundan Krishnan, SP Saran with Arjun Lal, Sub Inspector of Police and 
Constable Jitendra Singh took the decision to climb the jail wall. Led by the SP, these police personnel facing a mob of stone 
being thrown by aggressive prisoners managed to scale the jail wall and get a foothold on the roof of the administrative building 
of the jail adjoining the wall. From this location, an appeal was made to the stone pelting prisoners to go back into their wards 
and vacate the outer campus of the jail. However, the prisoners grew more violent and they began to throw stones and bombs, 
some firing was also started from which the police party escaped narrowly. The SP Saran got minor injuries on his legs and 
hand during this incident. Despite injuries the, SP Saran along with SI Arjun Lal led their subordinates and stood their position 
undeterred. The armed prisoners, turning a deaf ear to the warnings being given, continued their violent acts. They even 
threatened to blow up the main gate of the jail using gas cylinders which they had brought to the jail gate. They proceeded to 
break the western wall of the jail adjoining the police barracks. Sensing their intention, the SP Saran directed the police 
personnel to start effective firings in order to force the violent prisoners to retreat back into their barracks. The rioting prisoners 
were thus forced to withdraw to their barracks. The SP Saran then entered the jail compound by climbing down from the roof 
of the administrative building along with the few police personnel who were accompanying him. Then they removed the gas 
cylinders from the jail gate thus allowing for the use of welding machines to cut open the jammed main gate of the jail. As the 
Police parties entered into the jail premises, the armed prisoners positioned in the jail compound, began to throw stones and 
discharged a volley of fire on the entering police personnel. For some time, the entire jail compound was turned into a battle 
field. The defiant prisoners having no respect for the police and the administration were in no mood to surrender and acted like 
a gang of hardened criminals. Finding no alternative, the police personnel under the leadership of SP Saran started retaliatory 
firings. This situation continued for half an hour. Eventually, the retaliatory action by the police forced the aggressive prisoners 
to stop firing and surrender. At about 14.30 hours, during a search made by the magistrates deputed with the police, the bodies 
of 4(four) prisoners were found along with arms and live cartridges. Another 7(seven) prisoners were found injured and were 
removed to hospital where another two prisoners later succumbed to their injuries. The gravity of the situation can be judged 
from the fact that a total of 48 tear gas shells, three tear gas hand grenades and 234 rounds were fired by the police during the 
above action. 


The leadership displayed by Kundan Krishnan, SP Saran and SI Arjun Lal, during this violent episode which 
continued for 3-4 hours, highlights their commitment to the call of duty and their act of bravery in a most adverse situation. SP 
Saran along with his subordinates, facing the maximum risk of life, displayed qualitative leadership from the front. Both the 
above officers by dint of their indomitable courage and determination, prevented a violent jailbreak by over a thousand 
prisoners. 


In this operation, S/Shri Kundan Krishnan, IPS, Superintendent of Police, Arjun Lal, Sub-Inspector and Jitendra Singh, 
Constable of Bihar Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


PART I—Sec.1] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) 251 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 30/03/2002. 

SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 41-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Delhi Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Amit Nara Inspector GM 

2. Braj Pal Singh Kushwaha Sub-Inspector GM 

3. Satish Kumar Sub-Inspector GM 

4. Udham Singh Assistant Sub-Inspector GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 26/11/2023, source information was received about two sharpshooters linked to the designated KTF terrorist and 
notorious international gangster Arshdeep Singh Arsh Dala, planning a clandestine meeting near Mayur Vihar, Delhi to procure 
weapons and money. Inspector Amit Nara, SI Satish Kumar, ASI Udham and SI Braj Pal Singh Kushwaha, along with other 
police officials, swiftly mobilized to intercept the suspects. 


Arriving at the scene, the officers positioned themselves strategically to confront the suspects. When the suspects 
attempted to flee on a motorcycle, SI Braj Pal Singh Kushwaha, in police uniform, stepped forward to halt them. Despite his 
commands, the suspects recklessly attempted to escape, resulting in a tumultuous chase and eventual confrontation. 


In a desperate bid to escape, the culprits resorted to indiscriminate gunfire, aiming recklessly at the advancing police 
team in which the bullets found their mark on the front side of Inspector Amit Nara and ASI Udham Singh’s bulletproof jackets, 
a testament to their unwavering bravery. Undeterred by the hail of bullets, Inspector Amit, ASI Udham Singh, and SI Braj Pal 
Singh Kushwaha returned fire to neutralize the threat and protect their team members. 


Ina display of remarkable bravery, Inspector Amit Nara, SI Braj Pal Singh Kushwaha, SI Satish Kumar moved swiftly 
to apprehend suspects. Amid the struggle, Inspector Amit Nara, SI Braj Pal Singh Kushwaha managed to wrestle the pistol 
from the suspect’s grip. However, the situation escalated when the suspect reached into his bag, prompting Inspector Amit 
Nara to act decisively. With quick reflexes, he seized the suspects hand, thwarting his attempt to retrieve an object. Acting on 
instinct, Inspector Amit Nara swiftly disarmed the suspect, revealing the object to be a hand grenade, a potentially catastrophic 
weapon. Meanwhile, ASI Udham Singh, SI Satish Kumar successfully apprehended the second suspect and secured the firearm 
used by the assailant, ensuring the safety of all involved and averting a potentially devastating outcome. 


Through their valorous efforts, Inspector Amit Nara, SI Satish Kumar, ASI Udham Singh, and SI Braj Pal Singh 
Kushwaha displayed exceptional bravery and commitment to duty, risking their lives to uphold the law and safeguard the 
community. 


In this operation, S/Shri Amit Nara, Inspector, Braj Pal Singh Kushwaha, Sub-Inspector, Satish Kumar, Sub-Inspector 
and Udham Singh, Assistant Sub-Inspector of Delhi Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a 
higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 26/11/2023. 

SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 42-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Delhi Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Manoj Kumar Inspector GM 

2. Anshu Chaudhary Sub-Inspector GM 


252 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec.1 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
3. Aleem Ahmad Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 08/08/2024, on the basis of secret information that Rizwan Ali R/o Daryaganj, Delhi, a terrorist wanted by NIA 
and having reward of Rs. 3 Lakh on his head, is moving around Faridabad- Delhi border area to carry out some criminal activity, 
he would be coming near Tughlaqabad Biodiversity Park, Delhi. He also possesses sophisticated illegal arms ammunition, a 
raiding team was constituted including Insp. Manoj Kumar, SI Anshu Chaudhary, HC Aleem Ahmad and others. At around 
10:10 PM, raiding team reached at the place of information. Team was briefed and deployed at the strategic points in and 
around the place of information. 


At around 11:25 PM, one person came on a black color splendor motorcycle from Mehrauli Badarpur Road and moved 
on Ma Anandmayi Marg. Informer identified the rider as wanted terrorist Rizwan Ali. In the meantime, suspected person turned 
left towards Chaudhary Ganga Baksh Marg. Sensing the police presence, he tried to flee towards Tuglaqabad Village but seeing 
police team from the front also, he slowed down his bike and jumped off from bike and started running towards the road side 
wall. 


Insp. Manoj Kumar, SI Anshu Chaudhary, who were chasing the suspect in Scorpio car, swiftly got down from the 
running car. HC Aleem Ahmad, who was coming from the front, also got off the motorcycle and tried to surround the suspect. 
Police team repeatedly shouted “POLICE-POLICE, HUM POLICE HAI, RUK JA” and asked him to surrender but instead of 
surrendering, he took out a pistol and fired indiscriminately towards the police party and tried to flee. Being in the direction 
line of fire, one bullet each hit the bullet proof jacket worn by Insp. Manoj Kumar and SI Anshu Chaudhary fired by accused 
Rizwan. 


Despite being hit on the bullet proof jacket, in order to apprehend the suspect and for the safety of the team members 
Insp. Manoj, SI Anshu Chaudhary and HC Aleem Ahmad also fired in self- defense. Suspect tried to climb the road side wall 
to jump other side in order to escape. But Insp. Manoj, SI Anshu Chaudhary and HC Aleem Ahmad showed tremendous 
presence of mind, exceptional courage and without caring for their lives, pounced over the Rizwan Ali. Without caring for 
one’s life, SI Anshu Chaudhary and HC Aleem Ahmad overpowered Rizwan Ali after a brief scuffle, while Insp. Manoj Kumar 
snatched loaded pistol from the right hand of Rizwan Ali. Pistol was found loaded having two live cartridges in its magazine 
and one live cartridge in its chamber. During the exchange of fire, total five rounds were fired by the police party in self- 
defense. 


The brave action of Insp. Manoj Kumar, SI Anshu Chaudhary and HC Aleem Ahmad won great applause not only 
from the highest ranks of police and Media, but also from the public at large. Undeterred and unfazed, they bravely confronted 
this desperate terrorist without caring for their personal lives despite having no cover while apprehending this dreaded terrorist 
as they were in direct line of fire and showed indomitable courage, dedication, presence of mind and a rare gallant act. 


In this operation, S/Shri Manoj Kumar, Inspector, Anshu Chaudhary, Sub-Inspector and Aleem Ahmad, Head 
Constable of Delhi Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 08/08/2024. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 43-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Delhi Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Pramod Singh Kushwah Additional Commissioner of Police GM 

2. Rajeev Kumar Sub-Inspector GM 
Shibu R.S Sub-Inspector GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 04.01.2024, a reliable secret input was received that an “A” category terrorist, member of banned terrorist outfit 
Hizbul Mujahideen (HM), carrying a reward of 10 Lakh and wanted in 11 terror cases of Jammu and Kashmir, including killing 


PART I—Sec.1] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) 253 


of 5 security personnel, attack on the residence of SP Sopore with RPG and hand grenade, IED blast at PS Sopore and 4 
incidents of grenade attack at PS Sopore, CRPF camp at SBI complex, Hathishah, BSNL office Sopore and Chankhan Sopore, 
namely Javed Ahmed Mattu @ Irshad Ahmed Malla @Ehsan S/o Abdul Gani Mattu R/o Khushal Mattu, Old Town, Sopore, 
J&K is going to take the delivery of huge amount of arms, ammunitions and hand grenade near DND Flyway, Delhi in a white 
Santro Car and he will execute any major terrorist strikes in J&K and other part of country. As per the input, a trap was laid 
around the DND Flyway. A raiding team was constituted. Based on the input, a trap was laid around the DND Flyway, and a 
raiding team was constituted. Sh. Pramod Singh Kushwah, Addl. CP alongwith SI Rajeev Kumar and SI Shibu R.S, took 
positions at the DND Flyway and Mayur Vihar loop. 


At about 04:00 PM, one White color Santro Car was seen coming from Mayur Vihar Toll Plaza towards DND flyway. 
The driver of the alleged car was identified by the informer as Javed Ahmed Mattu. Sh. Pramod Singh Kushwah alongwith SI 
Anshu shouted “Police-Police Ruko-Ruko” and signaled him to stop. After chasing him down for half a kilometre, his car was 
overtaken and stopped. Javed Ahmed Mattu exited from the car and started running towards the Pusta. Sh. Pramod Singh 
Kushwah, Addl. CP alongwith SI Rajeev Kumar and SI Shibu R.S chased him. Upon seeing the approaching police party, 
Javed Ahmed Mattu started firing indiscriminately with the intention to kill them and tried to escape from the spot. Thereafter, 
in self-defence Sh. Pramod Singh Kushwah, Addl. CP alongwith SI Rajeev Kumar and SI Shibu R:S fired 3,3 and 2 round 
respectively from their govt. pistol. During the brief exchange of fire, Sh. Pramod Singh Kushwah, Addl. CP suffered gunshot 
injury on his bullet proof Jacket. Sh. Pramod Singh Kushwah, SI Rajeev Kumar and SI Shibu R.S overpowered Javed Ahmed 
Mattu while he was firing on police party. Sh. Pramod Singh Kushwah snatched the loaded pistol from Javed Ahmed Mattu 
after a brief scuffle. 


Their gallant action, not only won appreciation from the higher ranks in police and print visual media but also from 
the public at large. Undeterred and unfazed, they put their life at risk and bravely confronted this desperate terrorist and showed 
indomitable courage, dedication, presence of mind and a rare gallant act. The brave and timely action of the above officers 
averted a major terrorist strike in Delhi various parts of country and saved several innocent lives. 


In this operation, S/Shri Pramod Singh Kushwah, Additional Commissioner of Police, Rajeev Kumar, Sub-Inspector 
and Shibu R.S, Sub-Inspector of Delhi Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 04/01/2024. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 44-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Delhi Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Nishant Dahiya Inspector GM 

2, Manjit Jaglan Sub-Inspector GM 

3. Amit Bhati Sub-Inspector GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On the intervening night of 11-12/10/2023, the team of Counter Intelligence, Special Cell, Delhi received specific 
information regarding movements of two sharpshooters associated with Canada based fugitive and designated terrorist 
Arshdeep Singh Arsh Dalla, in Delhi. Following a meticulous strategic planned field operation, both suspects were intercepted 
on Ring Road, North of Sarai Kale Khan, in the midnight of October 11 12102023. However, when the team challenged them 
to stop, one of the accused opened fire towards the police team, which narrowly missed SI Amit Bhati and ASI Naveen Kumar 
from an extremely close range, leaving the entire team completely off guard. Simultaneously, another suspect took out a live 
high explosive Hand Grenade from his possession and started putting efforts to unpin it in order to cause harm to the police 
team. 


The consequences of such an action could have been catastrophic, resulting in severe harm to the team members, the 
locals, and even the target themselves. Nevertheless, demonstrating exceptional courage and disregard for their own safety, 
Inspector Nishant Dahiya, Sub-Inspector Manjit Jaglan, and Sub-Inspector Amit Bhati displayed exceptional courage, 
selflessness and disregard for their own safety. In a coordinated act of heroism, they swiftly reacted to apprehend the culprits. 


SI Amit Bhati demonstrating immense bravery, took the lead by strategically drawing the criminals' attention through 
a bold challenge and advancement, putting himself directly in the line of fire, allowing his team to respond effectively. 


254 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec.1 


Simultaneously, Sub-Inspector Manjit Jaglan with the help of the notice team overpowered the culprit, who was trying 
to detonate the hand grenade and heroically snatched the live device. 


Meanwhile, Inspector Nishant Dahiya alongside his team members, charged towards the armed assailant, who had 
already fired one round at the police team and thereby seizing the opportunity to fire another round. 


The gallant act of the team during this livewire armed confrontation exemplifies the highest standards of bravery and 
selflessness. The presence of mind applied and swift action taken by Inspector Nishant Dahiya and Sub-Inspector Manjit Jaglan, 
averted potential disaster. 


Thereafter, all requisite legal and procedural formalities were duly executed. A case under provisions of relevant penal 
sections of IPC & Arms Act was registered and the duo were accordingly arrested. 


In this operation, S/Shri Nishant Dahiya, Inspector, Manjit Jaglan, Sub-Inspector and Amit Bhati, Sub-Inspector of 
Delhi Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 12/10/2023. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 45-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Delhi Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
1. Shri Krishan Kumar Inspector GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On dated 14.01.2024 in a major crackdown, a desperate interstate contract killer & sharpshooter of Kala Jathedi - Raju 
Basodi - Anil Chippi Gang named Vicky @ Kartoos, 29 years R/o VPO - Sanghi, District Rohtak, Haryana was arrested after 
a brief exchange of fire at Chhawla Drain, Delhi. Accused was wanted in a murder case of PS Kotkashim, Rajasthan, 02 
sensational cases of extortion of Dwarka District, Delhi and also wanted as source of weapon in two cases of firing in Delhi. 
Miscreant fired 04 rounds upon the police team, two bullets hit the person of Insp. Krishan Kumar but luckily, he worn BP 
jacket. In order to self-defence, police fired 06 rounds on assailant, 02 bullets hit the right leg of the accused and he was 
apprehended. In this regard, a case vide FIR No. 16/24 dated 14.01.2024 u/s 186/353/307 IPC 25/27 Arms Act has been 
registered at PS Crime Branch. 


Accused Vicky @ Kartoosh was running the extortion racket of Kala Jathedi Gang on ground. On 09.01.2024, accused 
Vicky @ Kartoosh sent 02 shooters Ashish Sahil and Krishan at Shiv properties, Old Palam Road, Delhi. They fired on the 
property dealer’s office and handed over extortion demand letter to him and demanded 1.5 Crores in the name of Kala Jathedi 
and Raju Basodi. 9 empty cartridges and one chit demanding 1.5 Crores were recovered from the spot. In this regard, case FIR 
No. 14/24 u/s 307/387/34 IPC 25/27 Arms Act, PS Dwarka North has been registered. 


In November 2023, accused Vicky @ Kartoos was hired by one lawyer namely Satpal to kill his rival person Aman. 
On the instructions of Satpal, he killed Aman in a marriage function on dance floor. During this incident, one Army personnel 
and one UP Police personnel also sustained bullet injuries when they tried to save the life of victim. In this regard, FIR No. 
489/23 dated 29.11.2023 u/s 302/307/143/341/323 IPC & 3/25 Arms Act was registered at PS Kotkaism, Rajasthan. 


In another incident, he directed his two shooters namely Kartik and Pardeep to extort money from property dealers. 
On his instruction, on 07.11.2023, accused Kartik and Pardeep fired on the office of owner of the Gaurav Homes Property 
Dealer. They also dropped a handwritten letter at the office in which, they had asked him to meet Dinesh Karala Bhai and also 
threatened that if he did not meet Dinesh Bhai, bullet would pierce the chest of the person, found sitting inside the office and 
grenades would be thrown at their construction sites. They demanded Rs. 2 crores from property dealer. While escaping from 
the spot, shooters fired several rounds in the air to terrorize the public. In this regard, a case FIR No. 737/23 u/s 307/384/34 
IPC & 25/27 Arms Act was registered at PS Bindapur, Delhi. Both the shooters Kartik and Pardeep were arrested by Crime 
Branch with 02 sophisticated pistols and cartridges in case FIR No. 276/23 dated 23.11.2023 u/s Arms Act, PS Crime Branch, 
Delhi. The arms used in the case were provided by Vicky@ Kartoos to extort money from the property dealer. Crime Branch 
was searching accused Vicky @ Kartoos as the source of illegal pistols and ammunitions in the said case. Keeping in view the 
sensitivity of the matter, the team of Anti Gangs Squad, Crime Branch was tasked to work upon the suspect. Manual as well as 
technical surveillance was mounted on suspects. Thereafter, secret input was received on 14.01.2024, that a suspect who is 
involved in the extortion case of PS Dwarka North and PS Bindapur and murder case of PS Kotkasim, Alwar, Rajasthan would 
come Near Chhawla Ganda Nala Crossing Road, Chhawla, Delhi to meet his associates for supplying illegal arms and

ammunition. It was also informed that he always carries fire arms/weapons and does not hesitate in opening fire upon police 
team. Accordingly, reacting on the information, a team led by Insp. Krishan Kumar comprising SI Sachin Pulia, ASI Surender 
Kumar, HC Deepak, HC Dharamraj, HC Pappu, HC Mintu and HC Shyam Sunder under the close supervision of ACP Naresh 
Kumar was constituted by DCP Amit Goel to nab the gangster/shooter. The team reached near Chhawla-Bijwasan Crossing, 
Chhawla Drain Road, Delhi and a picket checking was started. On 14.01.2024, at around 02:45 AM, one motorcycle was 
intercepted which was coming from Chawla Drain Road, Delhi and was signaled to stop by the police officials, on which he

two bullets hit on the right leg of of miscreant and police team managed to apprehend the accused Vicky @ Kartoos with huge 
consignment of arms and ammunitions. 


In this operation, Shri Krishan Kumar, Inspector of Delhi Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 11.07.2022, Awantipora Police received an information regarding movement of terrorists on Padgampora Goripora 
Road. The input was analysed/corroborated and immediately shared with CO 50th RR and CO 130th Bn CRPF. Upon this, a 
joint cordon and search operation was launched by Awantipora Police alongwith CRPF 130th Bn., Army 50 RR at Wandakpora 
Goripora crossing area. Besides this, deployment of splinter coy F/152 Bn CRPF already deployed in the area at lateral, Naka 
points in connection with Shri Amarnath Ji Yatra-2022 was also put on alertness. 


As per evolved strategy, the composite party of Awantipora Police, Army 50 RR and CRPF approached the suspected 
area by adopting the unconventional treacherous routes maintaining high level of surprise till gap free cordon get established. 
After laying & strengthening the cordon, the steps for search of terrorist in the suspected area was started by the joint team of 
Police Awantipora led by Shri Mumtaz Ali- SDPO Awantipora alongwith his buddies with the assistance of counter part of 
Army 50 RR, CRPF 130 Bn/splinter Coy F/152 Bn. CRPF. Consequently, three teams were constituted one lead by DySP 
Javaid Ahmad Lone alongwith ASI Davinder Kumar, HC Shabeer Ah. Narwaroo, HC Bashir Ahmad, Const. Mushtaq Ahmad, 
Sanjev Singh on Northern side of suspected area alongwith counter part of Army 50 RR & 130 Bn CRPF, another party led by 
DySP Mumtaz Ali alongwith Inspr. Sameer Ahmad, SgCt. Zahoor Ahmad, HC Yaseen Ahmad, SgCt. Rakesh Kumar, SgCt. 
Rattan, Const. Gulzar Ahmad, SPO Irshad Ahmad on Eastern side alongwith counter part of Army 50 RR & CRPF 130 Bn & 
splinter Coy 152nd Bn CRPF on Western side & 3rd party under the command of Inspr. Younis Khan alongwith HC Harjeet 
Singh, HC Rajinder Singh, SPO Mir Saleem, SPO Sheikh Muzaffar Ahmad and SPO Sajad Ahmad alongwith buddies of Army 
& CRPF 130 Bn/152 Bn CRPF on Eastern side. 


The whole suspected area was fully cordoned off and exhaustive search and frisking of all commuters was started at 
the entire axis leading towards the NHW-44 and other areas in the vicinity. During search and frisking by joint team of Police/ 
Army/ CRPF, two persons on a bike while they were moving on Padgampora-Kakpora road were signalled to stop near 
Wandakpora-Goripora crossing but they opened indiscriminate fire upon the Police/ SFs with the intention to kill them with 
their illegal arms/ammunition and started fleeing towards vegetation area in the nearby fields towards village Wandakpora. The 
assault party of Police/Army/CRPF immediately took positions & retaliated in self-defence to overpower the terrorists and gun

battle started between the joint team of search/assault party of Police/ Army/ CRPF & terrorists from the close range. Since the 
area was full of shrubs, trees, wooden blocks lying in abundance and highly undulated ground, the terrorists took positions 
there but the joint assault party of Police/ Army/ CRPF effectively started engaging the terrorists in gun battle. During the gun 
battle the hiding terrorists opened volley of fire upon the search/assault party coupled with hurling of hand grenades and tried 
to break the cordon and get escaped. The composite assault party of Police Awantipora, 50-RR, CRPF 130 Bn/ splinter Coy 
F/152 CRPF spearheaded by DySP Javid Ahmad Lone alongwith his buddies took cover and fought bravely from the front 
without caring for their lives and neutralized both the terrorists on the spot. The slain terrorists were later on identified as Kaisar 
Rashid Koka (AGuH later joined JeM outfit, C-category) S/o Ab. Rashid Koka R/o Kaigam Awantipora and Ishfaq Ahmad 
Lone S/o Gh Nabi Lone R/o Lelhar Pulwama (JeM outfit, C-category). Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession 
of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 160/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 16, 18 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S 
Awantipora. The slain terrorist Kaiser Rashid Koka was highly Pak trained terrorist. He went to Pakistan for acquiring illegal 
arms/ammunition training and returned with Arms/ammunition to wage war against the country/UT-J&K. The operation 
concluded without causing any collateral damage. 


In this operation, S/Shri Javaid Ahmad Lone, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Younis Khan, Inspector, Davinder 
Kumar, Assistant Sub-Inspector, Shabeer Ah. Narwaroo, Head Constable, Rakesh Kumar, SgCT and Rattan Lal, SgCT of

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 04.10.2022 at about 2005 hrs, Police Shopian generated an intelligence input about the presence of terrorists in 
village Drach Keegam of District Shopian who were planning to carry out major terror activities in the area. On this, Police 
Shopian launched an operation in collaboration with 44 RR, 14th BN CRPF without getting panic or situation gets worsened. 


Accordingly, a team of Police Shopian under the overall supervision of SSP Tanushree including DySP Raja Majid 
Butt, HC Javid Ahmad, HC Gayour-Ul-Haq, SgCt Rouf Ahmad, SgCt Ubaidullah Manzoor, SgCt Tariq Rasool, Const.

Ahmad, SPO Qaiser Ahmad, SPO Shamsheer Husain & SPO Wasim Ahmad alongwith team of 44 RR & 14th BN CRPF were 
formed to start search of target area. Police/SFs also ensured tactical deployment of all teams of Police/Security forces, besides, 
placement of Bullet proof vehicles at all the vents and possible escape routes. However, during the spot interrogation of 
occupants namely Shakeel Ahmad & Bilal Ahmad both sons of Gh. Hassan Bhat R/o Drach, Keegam who were found a bit 
nervous confirmed presence of three suspected terrorists in their single-story building. Initially some houses and a bit area 
adjoining to the target area was brought under the cordon tactfully by initial joint cordon team under the overall supervision of 
SSP Shopian. 

During search operation, target area was pinpointed/zeroed by the search teams to locate the terrorists. Afterwards, 
Police teams headed by DySP Raiz Ahmad including Inspr. Atal Sharma, ASI Reyaz Ahmad, SgCt. Zahoor Ahmad, SgCt. 
Irfan Ahmad, Const. Mohmad Hussain, Const. Zulfikaq Ali, SgCt. Tariq Rasool, Const. Mohmad Saleem Dar, Const. 
Obedullah Manzoor, Const. Firdous Ahmad, Const. Mohd Rafiq, Const. Vicky Khajuria, Const. Maneet Sharma, Const. Jasvir 
Singh, Const. Ajay Kumar, Const. Farooq Ahmad, Const. Reshav Kumar, Const. Rahul Kumar, SPO Rafiq Ahmad and SPO 
Asif Mushtaq were formed for final assault to eliminate the holed-up terrorists. 


Before launching final assault, besieged terrorists were asked to surrender, which they declined and instead opened 
heavy volume of fire towards security forces. At this moment, one terrorist was noticed moving along with AK like weapon in 
the targeted house. Seeing his movement, few probing shots were given by DySP Raiz Ahmad and his team which was retaliated 
fiercely by terrorists. But terrorists were manoeuvring their positions from one room to another room. During firing, terrorists 
tried to flee from the house & their escape movement was clearly visible. On seeing movement of terrorists, DySP Raja Majid

No. 48-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
1. Shri Reyaz Ahmad SgCT GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 15.06.2023, based on a credible information from own sources, about terrorists’ infiltration involving a mixed 
group of Peoples Anti-Fascist Force (PAFF) and Ghaznavi Force in the border area of Jumagund of Police Station Trehgam, a 
joint search operation was initiated. The District Police Kupwara and Army 04 SIKH collaborated under the supervision of 
DIG Vivek Gupta. Despite challenging terrain and dense forests near the LoC, the Police teams led by SSP Yougal Kumar, 
DySP Sarfaraz Bashir and DySP Mohammad Rafee alongwith Army 04 SIKH cordoned off the area and executed a coordinated 
search operation at about! 800 hours. 


During the intervening night of June 15-16, 2023, at around 0130 hours, the search parties observed terrorist movement 
in the dense forest. As the searching parties started closing in, the terrorists initiated heavy gunfire from different directions 
upon the search parties, who responded in a controlled manner to prevent causalities and maintain the cordon. The parties 
followed the devised plan and gradually closed in on the targets and engaged the terrorists till the dawn to avoid any collateral 
damage. 


As the party/column led by SSP Yougal Kumar approached the expected target, it encountered intense gunfire from 
the terrorists hiding in the thick vegetation of the forest. In the face of danger, amidst a chaotic and perilous situation, the 
column led by SSP Kupwara maneuvered through the chaos and fearlessly without caring for their personal safety charged at 
the hiding terrorists and eliminated two (02) heavily armed terrorists at a close range. 


Simultaneously, the parties led by DySP Sarfaraz Bashir along with SgCt Reyaz Ahmad, SgCt Altaf Ahmad and DySP 
Mohd Rafee alongwith HC Tanveer Ahmad, HC Kishor Kumar and SgCt Jahangir Ahmad respectively located the other three 
hiding terrorists and jointly neutralized them in a swift and professional manner. The operation resulted in the successful 
elimination of five (05) foreign heavily armed terrorists affiliated with banned terror organizations Ghaznavi Force and PAFF. 
This accomplishment was achieved despite challenging topography and hostile forests, all while avoiding collateral damage. 
The eliminated terrorists affiliated with banned terror organizations Ghaznavi Force/PAFF were later-on identified as Abdali 
@ Shamsheer Pathan, Gazafi @ Sheer Khan R/o Rawlakot Pok, Sayan @ Ayoob, Janbaz @ Waheed R/o Pansheer Afgan and 
Waseem @ Talha R/o Bagh Pok. Huge cache of Arms/ammunition and other items were recovered from the possession of the 
slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 37/2023 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA. Act & 16, 20, 23, 38 UA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Trehgam 
Kupwara for further investigation. 


258 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec.1 


For exceptional bravery and valiant actions in the face of extreme danger, the police team specially Shri Reyaz Ahmad, 
SgCT displayed remarkable courage and unyielding determination during the operation. Their selflessness, quick thinking and 
unwavering commitment to the mission resulted in the successful neutralization of five (05) terrorists, thereby safeguarding the 
lives of fellow comrades and innocent civilians. 


In this operation, Shri Reyaz Ahmad, SgCT of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage 
and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 07.06.2022 at check Nutnussa Kupwara, based on a reliable information provided by own sources, a joint operation 
was launched by District Police Kupwara and Army 47 RR under the supervision of DIG Udaya Bhaskar Billa and SSP 
Kupwara. 02 Police search parties one headed by DySP Aijaz Ahmad alongwith SgCt Ishtiaq Ahmad Khan and other 
spearheaded by Inspr. Mohd Rafiq alongwith SgCt Mohd Youns, Ct Safeer Lone and other PC Nafri were constituted on the 
directions of DIG Baramulla assisted by other forces, cordoned the area and started the search operation of the suspected area. 
Since the area being highly vulnerable in view of dense forests and upper reach search operation by the joint forces of Police 
Kupwara and army was launched effectively. 


The terrorists as per intelligence input were hiding in the forest and it was highly challenging for the Police party viz. 
DySP Ajaz Ahmad, Inspr. Mohd Rafiq, SgCt Mohd Youns Khan, SgCt. Ishtiaq Ahmed Khan, Ct. Safeer Lone and other police 
officials and army to locate him. The search operation was extended in various areas and the police party and army without 
caring for their personal efforts moved forward and noticed the movement of a terrorists. The terrorists on seeing the police 
party & army fired upon the joint forces with their illegally acquired automatic weapons and heavy exchange of fire took place 
between Army/ Police and terrorists. Mohd Rafiq, Inspector, Ishtiaq Ahmed Khan, SgCT and Safeer Lone, Constable keep 
fighting bravely being part of first cordon until the elimination of 02 dreadful terrorists whose identity were later on ascertained 
as Tufail (A- category) R/o Pak and Ishtiyaq Farooq Lone (C-category) S/o Faroog Ahmad Lone R/o Lone Mohalla Reshipora 
Tral District Pulwama. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 148/2022 U/S 
307 IPC, 7/27 1.A.Act, 13, 16 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Kupwara for further investigation. 


In the entire operation, Mohd Rafiq, Inspector, Ishtiaq Ahmed Khan, SgCT and Safeer Lone, Constable exhibited 
professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by them as advance team, who fought bravely without caring 
for their lives was remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in extremely hostile situation was truly gallant. Although 
the terrorist tried their best to cause damage to the forces by resorting to indiscriminate firing in which police team especially 
Mohd Rafiq, Inspector, Ishtiaq Ahmed Khan, SgCT and Safeer Lone, Constable had a very narrow escape. However, they 
without losing their concentration and in good application of mind fought bravely which led to neutralization of 02 dreadful 
foreign/ local terrorists. 


In this operation, S/Shri Mohd Rafiq, Inspector, Ishtiaq Ahmed Khan, SgCT and Safeer Lone, Constable of Jammu & 
Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

No. 50-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) and Ist Bar to GM to the 
under mentioned personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police: — 


81. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Javaid Ahmad Lone Deputy Superintendent of Police 15 Bar to GM 
2. Younis Khan Inspector 15 Bar to GM 
3. Sameet Pal Singh Head Constable GM 

4. Umar Nazir SgCT GM 

5. Ab. Rashid Lone Constable GM 

|6. | Reyaz Ahmad Sheikh Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 01.11.2022, Awantipora Police received information regarding the conspiracy of terrorists to carry out a fidayeen 
attack in Awantipora area. The intelligence input so generated got shared with Army CO 55 RR and keeping in view all the 
hazards in consideration, a fool proof strategy got devised that include placement of joint MVCP (Naka), teams in coverts, 
QRTs at nearby locations to meet out immediate requirement etc. 


As per preconceived devised strategy immediately a joint MVCP (Naka) by Police Awantipora and Army 55 RR was 
established at Khandipora Renzipora link road and search of all commuters and vehicles were started. During checking of 
vehicles, one vehicle (Alto car) was signaled to stop. However, the said vehicle did not stop and tried to run away from above 
Naka point. However, another joint team of Police and Army, who were approx. 30 mtrs ahead from former Naka (MVCP); 
the team B was comprising of one Army Maj of 55 RR alongwith three others with him and SSP Mohd Yousif, HC Farooq 
Ah., HC Javid Ahmad, SgCt. Umar Nazir & DySP Javaid Ahmad Lone, HC Sameet Pal Singh, Ct. Ab. Rashid Lone & Ct 
Reyaz Ahmad Sheikh immediately swung into action and stretched road blocking chain with spikes. The fast driven vehicle 
tried to trespass this road barrier too but its frontal tyres were punctured and become immobilized. 


Subsequently, the inboard terrorist jumped out of so immobilized vehicle and showered a burst of bullets upon the 
above parties and tried to escape from the spot. The fire was immediately retaliated in self-defense triggering an encounter. 
One terrorist, among the three got neutralized in initial fire fight. While as remaining two terrorists took shelter in nearby field 
area in bushes. Both the teams keeping in view the position and fire angle of terrorists, stealthy crawled and occupied the 
strategic advantage location. The reinforced QRTs were also immediately pressed into service to cordon the whole area to 
negate escape of terrorists; these two QRTs have also plugged off all the possible escape routes. The hiding terrorists were 
again spotted by QRT tena with the aid of night vision device who were hiding in a highly undulated area. The terrorists were 
intermittently firing upon above joint operational parties. However, the fire was retaliated but due to hiding of terrorists in 
undulated ground area and woods, the fire was not effective. In this process of change in position, the hiding terrorists besides 
firing also lobbed grenade but that missed the intended location. Hence both team A & B occupied the tactically advantageous 
points, then both teams in exceptional synergy conjointly engage the terrorists in fire fight. 


During fierce gun fight the hiding terrorists, who were battle hardened got neutralized. The ever-resolute composite 
assault party of Police Awantipora & 55 RR fought bravely from the front without caring for their lives and neutralized all the 
three terrorists. Dead bodies of 03 terrorists were recovered from the encounter site, whose identity were later on ascertained 
as Mukhtar Ahmad Bhat (B-category) S/o Mohammad Magqbool Bhat R/o Kakapora Pulwama, Mushfiq @ Ali @ Mubashir 
(A-category) R/o Pak and Saqlain Mushtaq Ganie (Nick Name-Gazanfar) S/o Mushtaq Ah.Ganie R/o Koil Pulwama all 
affiliated with LeT outfit. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. A case FIR No. 237/2022 
U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 1.A.Act, 3/4 Exp.Subs Act, 16, 18 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Awantipora and investigation 
taken-up. 

In the entire operation DySP Javaid Ahmad Lone, Inspr. Younis Khan, HC Sameet Pal Singh, SgCt. Umar Nazir, Ct. 
Reyaz Ahmad Sheikh and Ct. Ab. Rashid Lone were oblivious to imminent danger but had played a conspicuous role of 
bravery, courage, rare tactical acumen and exemplary jointmenship, unparalleled courage and selfless devotion to duty which 
led to the successful neutralization of three dreaded terrorists without suffering any collateral damage.

Singh, Head Constable, Umar Nazir, SgCT, Ab. Rashid Lone, Constable and Reyaz Ahmad Sheikh, Constable of Jammu &

No. 51-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Shabir Nawab Senior Superintendent of Police GM 

2 Mohammad Shafi Magray Head Constable GM 

3. Mohd Ramzan Rather Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 31.08.2022 at about 1930 hours, while acting upon a credible information about the presence of JeM terrorists in 
area of Reshipora-Bomai, who are planning to carryout attack on security forces to harm the Police/SFs, accordingly a joint 
cordon and search operation was launched in the area by Sopore Police, in collaboration with 22 RR and 179 Bn. CRPF. As 
the area is densely-populated and it was hard to zero in the terrorists in evening hours due to darkness, there were chances of 
civilian causalities as the terrorists could take shelter in any residential house of area and could take the civilians as hostage, 
and use them as human shield. There was every possibility that terrorists can take advantage of darkness to escape from the 
hiding spot as such it was very difficult to neutralize the terrorists without any collateral damage, but the security forces adopted 
a prudent strategy and at first instance, the whole area was cordoned off and the people who were residing in nearby houses 
were evacuated from the area to the safer places and during the process of evacuation the security forces exhibited highest 
degree of professionalism and resilience by putting their lives at great risk. Once the civilian's evacuation process was 
completed, the cordon was tightened by sealing all entry and exit points, search operation started and during search operation, 
security forces fired few bullets in air to get the response from the terrorist’s side, but no response was heard initially. 


Accordingly, as per planned strategy and after thorough consideration and consultation with the counterparts of Army 
and CRPF, two joint teams, who volunteered themselves to move ahead towards the suspected place for last and final assault 
were formulated. The Ist joint team of Police/ Army/GRPF consisting of DySP Gazenfer Syed, Inspr. Zia-Ur-Rehman, Sl 
Javaid Hamid, HC Zubair Ahmad, HC Mohammad Shafi Magray, SgCt Nisar Ahmad, Const. Shabir Ahmad Beigh, Const. 
Mohd Ramzan Rather, Const. Sharief-ud-din, SPO Bilal Ahmad, SPO Javid Ahmad and SPO Mohd Rafiq headed by SSP 
Shabir Nawab was constituted as an advance team, who would do their assignment under the backup/covering firing of 2nd 
joint party including SI Yousuf-ul-Umar, ASI Gurjeet Singh, HC Omer Hussain, SgCt. Aashaq Hussain, Const. Zahid Hussain, 
Const. Bilal Ahmad, SPO Ashiq Hussain Dar, SPO Ishfaq Ahmad Baba, SPO Ishfaq Ahmad, SPO Irfan Ahmad headed by 
DySP Furqan Qadir. 


Team first started approaching in creeping formation towards the suspected spot and as soon as the joint team reached 
nearer to the suspected spot, the hiding terrorists fired indiscriminately with their sophisticated weapons upon the security 
forces, but the joint teams especially in team first SSP Shabir Nawab, HC Mohammad Shafi Magray and Const. Mohd Ramzan 
Rather remained determined and resilient just like a rock and retaliated the fire effectively face to face and don’t give them any 
chance to do any damage to security forces or chance of escaping from hiding spot and in the ensuing gunfight, two terrorists 
whose identity were later on ascertained as Mohd Rafi Lone S/o Ghuiam Mohammad Lone R/o Nathipora (B-Category) and 
Qaiser Ahmad Dar S/o Mohd Ashraf Dar R/o Tujjan Pulwama (C- Category) both belonging to JeM outfit got neutralized, and 
one civilian got minutely injured in cross firing whom terrorists tried to use as human shield but was evacuated professionally, 
he was later hospitalized and after providing first aid, he was discharged. While fighting with the slain terrorists, the joint teams 
especially in team first comprising SSP Shabir Nawab, HC Mohammad Shafi Magray and Const. Mohd Ramzan Rather put 
their lives at a great risk to uphold the security and sovereignty/ integrity of the country and it is because of their sheer bravery, 
resilience and quality leadership and professionalism, the operation was culminated with the elimination of two dreaded 
terrorists in a very difficult and uneven situation. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case 
FIR No. 43/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/271.A.Act, 18,20 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Bomai for further investigation. 


The siain terrorists were involved in a series of subversive activities and were involved in motivating youth to join 
into terrorist ranks within the jurisdiction of Police District Sopore. As far as local recruitment into terrorist ranks is concerned, 
their neutralization was considered a severe jolt to the JeM outfit and a big success for the security forces particularly Sopore 
Police. The area .of operation was densely populated surrounded by residential houses, which has always remained a target for 
the terrorists to attack security forces/ ROPs it was a big challenge for the security forces to launch an anti-terrorist operation 
in such an area without any collateral damage, but it was conspicuous bravery, leadership qualities, professionalism and 
matchless resilience exhibited by SSP Shabir Nawab, HC Mohammad Shafi Magray and Const. Mohd Ramzan Rather that the 
operation was concluded with the elimination of two dreaded terrorists without any collateral damage and as such these officers/ 
officials put their lives at a great risk in the instant operation to uphold the security/ sovereignty and integrity of the Nation. 


In this operation, S/Shri Shabir Nawab, Senior Superintendent of Police, Mohammad Shafi Magray, Head Constable 
and Mohd Ramzan Rather, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to 
duty of a higher order.

No. 52-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Ajit Dhar Assistant Sub-Inspector GM 

2. Ravi Kumar Head Constable GM 

3. Abid Ali Dar SgCT GM 

4. Sohail Ahmad Dar Constable GM 

5. Manzoor Ahmad Bhat Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 14.04.2022, Police Shopian received a specific input about the movement of terrorists of LeT outfit in village 
Badigam, Shopian. As per the input, these terrorists were in a process of carrying out major terror activities in the area to 
threaten the general public. The said village, which had been very vulnerable from terrorist point of view owing to its 
topography, support base to terrorists and as such conducting of operation in the area was a daunting task for Police/Security 
forces. Acting swiftly on the input, Police Shopian launched a joint operation with District Police Srinagar, 44 RR/ 178th Bn. 
CRPF and it was decided to lay a joint cordon to cover the target area from different directions under the overall supervision 
of SSP Amritpal Singh. Thereafter, teams of Police led by SSP Shopian alongwith SP Iftikhar Talib, ASI Ramesh Kumar, ASI 
Ajit Dhar, HC Ab. Qayoom Tantray, HC Harpal Singh, SgCt. Mohd Rafeed, SgCt. Parvaiz Ahmad, SgCt. Ayaz Ahmad, Ct. 
Amran Ahmad, Ct. Kapel Sharma, Ct. Sohail Ahmad Dar, SPO Sajad Ahmad, SPO Bashir Ahmad, SPO Abid Ahmad, SPO 
Rajan Kumar, SPO Yashpaul Singh alongwith 44 RR/178 Bn. CRPF were formed to start the search of target area. During the 
search operation, target area was pinpointed/zeroed by the searching teams. 


In the meantime, terrorists hiding in the target area sensing the movement of operational parties opened indiscriminate 
fire upon them with their illegal automatic weapons with intention to kill them and make their escape good. The search parties 
immediately took cover and retaliated with courage triggering a fierce gunfight. However, it was noticed that some civilians 
have got trapped in the cordoned area. The search parties led by SSP Shopian including the above personnel took the 
responsibility of evacuation of trapped civilians. After multiple attempts, they managed to rescue about 100 trapped civilians 
and moved them to secure locations. Afterwards, Police teams headed by SSP Shopian alongwith DySP Raiz Ahmed, HC 
Mohinder Singh, HC Bilal Ahmad, HC Ravi Kumar, SgCt. Ab. Majeed Dar, SgCt. Abid Ali Dar, SgCt. Manzoor Ahmad, SgCt. 
Aftab Ahmad, Ct. Guftar Ahmad, Ct. Ajit Kumar, Ct. Manzoor Ahmad Bhat, Foll. Parveen Singh Chib, SPO Salman Khan, 
SPO Raiz Hussain, SPO Shahnawaz Ahmad and SPO Riyaz Ahmad were formed for final assault to eliminate the holed-up 
terrorists. Before launching final assault, besieged terrorists were asked to surrender, which they refused and opened a heavy 
volume of fire and grenade lobbing towards security forces. In order to neutralize the hiding terrorists, above teams advanced 
towards the target alongwith QRTs of 44 RR and 178 Bn. CRPF covered the target from all directions and by crawling and 
moving tactfully forward, retaliated effectively with courage/bravery without caring for their lives from the front. Terrorists 
tried to escape from Eastern side during the face to face gunfight which ended into the elimination of 04 local terrorists (C- 
category) of LeT outfit, whose identity were later on ascertained as Aqib Ahmed Thoker S/o Farooq Ahmed Thoker R/o Heff- 
Kurri Zainapora Shopian, Showkeen Ahmad Mir S/o Mohammad Abdullah Mir R/o Sugan Zainapora, Waseem Ahmad Thoker 
@ Baku S/o Abdul Gani Thoker R/o Heff Kurri Zainapora and Farooq Ahmad Bhat S/o Abdul Salam Bhat R/o Sugan Draggad. 
Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 35/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 
16, 18, 20, 38 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Zainapora for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by ASI Ajit Dhar, HC Ravi Kumar, SgCt. Abid 
Ali Dar, Ct. Sohail Ahmad Dar and Ct. Manzoor Ahmad Bhat, who fought with terrorists efficiently and intelligently without 
caring for their lives was remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. 
They evacuated the civilians trapped in the cordoned area had undoubtedly averted the civil causalities by presence of mind 
and good policing. Although, terrorists tried their best to cause damage to the deployed forces by resorting to indiscriminate 
firing in which they all had a very narrow escape. However, they without losing their concentration, fought bravely and foiled 
their nefarious designs.

No. 53-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
1. Shri Malik Bashir-u-Din Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 19.02.2022 at about 0735 Hrs, Police Shopian received a specific intelligence input about presence of terrorists in 
village Cheermarg-Zainapora Shopian to carry out major terror activities in the area to threaten the general public. Accordingly, 
Police Shopian launched a joint operation in collaboration with Ist RR, 178th Bn. CRPF. While establishing the initial cordon, 
it was decided that civilians who were inside the village should be safely taken out from the inner cordon without getting panic 
or situation gets worsened. With the same drill, joint team under the overall supervision of SSP Amritpal Singh including DySP 
Raiz Ahmed, DySP Raja Majid Batt alongwith their QRTs and team of 15 Sector RR & 178th Bn. CRPF were formed to start 
the search of target area. During the spot interrogation of one occupant who was found a bit nervous, confirmed about the 
presence of one suspected terrorist hiding in the residential house. To avoid any untoward incident and collateral damage, 
targeted house was covered from different directions. Initially some houses and a bit area adjoining to the target area were 
brought under the cordon tactfully by initial joint cordon team under the overall supervision of SSP Shopian, including DySP 
Raja Majid Batt, HC Malik Bashir-u-Din, SgCt. Mushtaq Ahmad, Ct. Mohmad Saleem, Ct. Liyakat Ali SPO Anayat-u-llah, 
SPO Mohd Amin Ahanger and team of Ist RR & 178th Bn. CRPF were formed to start the search of target area. During the 
search operation, target area was pinpointed/zeroed by the searching teams. 


During the process of search, hiding terrorists fired indiscriminately upon the cordon parties. The joint troops retaliated 
the fire effectively and in exchange of fire, it was confirmed that one terrorist was holed up in the residential house. The 
besieged terrorist was asked to surrender through different means and given ample time for the same. However, he declined 
the offer and kept firing on the troops. The joint troops of Ist RR / 178 Bn. CRPF and DySP Raiz Ahmed including ASI Firdous 
Ahmad, HC Sameer Ahmad, HC Zahoor Ahmad, HC Ab Hamid, HC Bagh Hussain, HC Mohinder Singh, HC Malik Bashir- 
u-Din, SgCt. Murtaza Mughal, SgCt. Qaiser Ahmad, SgCt Manzoor Ahmad, SgCt. Mohd Amin, SgCt. Mushtaq Ahmad, SgCt. 
Abid Ali, Ct. Liyakat Ali, Ct. Manzoor Ahmad, SPO AnayatUllah, SPO Sajad Ahmad, SPO Raiz Hussain retaliated from the 
front and unfortunately two Army Jawans namely Sepoy Santosh Yadav and Sepoy Chavan Romit Tanaji of 1st RR got injured 
and later on, attained martyrdom in the said encounter. However, DySP PC Zainapora and his brave hearted team fired bullets 
from three sides on the terrorists and in firing, one terrorist was neutralized. After a prolonged gun battle, though there was no 
firing from the house which caught fire during encounter and was extinguished. During search, dead body of one killed terrorist 
along with his weapon was recovered. The slain terrorist was later on identified as Abdul Qayoom Dar S/o Late Gh. Mohi u 
Din Dar R/o Larve Kakapora, District Pulwama of LeT outfit. Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the possession of 
slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 15/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 16, 19, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Zainapora 
for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by HC Malik Bashir-u- Din who fought with 
terrorists efficiently and intelligently without caring for their precious lives was remarkable as the endeavor to reach to the 
target in extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. He evacuated the civilian trapped in the cordoned area thus evading civil 
casualties by presence of mind and good policing. Although the terrorist tried their best to cause damage the forces by resorting 
to indiscriminate firing in which he had a very narrow escape. However, HC Malik Bashir-u- Din without losing their 
concentration and in good application of mind, fought bravely and foiled their nefarious design. 


In this operation, Shri Malik Bashir-u-Din, Head Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous 
gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

No. 54-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) and Ist Bar to GM to the 
under mentioned personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police: — 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Mohd Ashrif Deputy Superintendent of Police 15 Bar to GM 

2. Sunil Raina Head Constable GM 

3. Imtiyaz Ahmad Mir Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 04.01.2022, Kulgam Police received specific information through reliable source regarding the presence of 
terrorists in village Okay Kulgam. Instantly Dr. G. V. Sundeep Chakravarthy, SSP Kulgam rushed towards the suspected village 
alongwith QRTs of District Police Kulgam/ Shopian, troops of 9 RR, 34 RR and contingents of CRPF 18th Bn to cordon off 
the whole village on war footing basis to ensure that the trapped terrorists will not get any chance to flee from the suspected 
village. The searching parties approached the residential house and questioned them regarding presence of terrorists but the 
family members strongly denied the presence of terrorists. However, the searching party moved on to conduct thorough search 
of the house, from where the hiding terrorists fired indiscriminately upon the searching parties so as to break the cordon in 
order to flee from the spot, but the fire was effectively retaliated by initial party ensuing to an encounter. As the encounter 
started, SSP Kulgam gave special priority to save the precious lives of civilians by evacuating them from the cordoned area 
towards safer places to ensure their safety and security first. 


After ensuring safety and security of civilians/villagers, SSP Kulgam chalked out a strategic plan to conduct the 
operation without any collateral damage. According to the strategic plan, three different search parties were constituted on spot 
to conduct door to door search. Ist search party headed by, SSP Kulgam alongwith ASI Manzoor Hussain, HC Rayees Ahmad, 
SgCt. Mohd Abass, Ct. Khurshid Ahmad, SPO Rahil Rashid, 2nd search party headed by DySP Gh. Mohd Bhat alongwith 
52९1. Abid Ahmad, SgCt. Tawfeeq Ahmad, Ct. Imtiyaz Ahmad Mir, Ct. Mohd Iqbal, SPO Mudasir Rehman and 3rd search 
party headed by DySP Sajad Ahmad, DySP Mohd Ashrif PC Imamsahib, ASI Firdous Ahmad, HC Sunil Raina, SgCt. Jahangeer 
Ahmad, SgCt. Maneer Ahmad, SPO Mohammad Masroor , SPO Mohd Ramzan. 


As the search party headed by Dr. G. V. Sundeep Chakravarthy, SSP Kulgam proceeded towards the suspected house 
and the hiding terrorists indiscriminately fired upon the searching party with the intention to kill them. SSP Kulgam alongwith 
his team retaliated and engaged in fierce combat with terrorists triggering to an encounter. The target house was surrounded 
from all sides and the hiding terrorists were given opportunity to surrender, which they refused. During gun battle, the brave 
officers of District Police Kulgam/ Shopian alongwith troops of 9 RR, 34 RR & contingents of CRPF 18th Bn killed two 
hardcore categorized terrorists on spot without any collateral damage by putting their lives at risk in the interest of security of 
state and National integrity. 


The coherence shown by the Police party especially DySP Mohd Ashrif, HC Sunil Raina and Ct. Imtiyaz Ahmad Mir, 
have played an exemplary role in elimination of 02 terrorists of LeT outfit namely Amir Ahmad Wani (Category “C’) S/o 
Abdul Satar R/o Alam Gunj District Shopian and Sameer Ahmad Khan (“C” Category) S/o Ab. Hamid Khan R/o Tiken 
Batapora Pulwama without caring about the safety of their lives. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain 
terrorists. In this regard, case FIR No. 03/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 13, 16, 19, 20 & 38 ULA (P) Act stands registered 
in P/S Kulgam. 


The death of terrorists was a big jolt to the structural and functional unit of banned terrorist organization “LeT”. The 
terrorists were also involved in motivating youth to join the terrorist ranks. Thus their objective was to vitiate peace and create 
a feeling of chaos among the pro-democratic people, thus injuring and hurting the basic sentinel of democracy. 


In this operation, S/Shri Mohd Ashrif, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Sunil Raina, Head Constable and Imtiyaz 
Ahmad Mir, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher 
order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 04/01/2022.

1. Mohan Lal Deputy Superintendent of Police 3rd Bar to GM 

Qs Abdul Rouf Wani SgCT 15 Bar to GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 10.04.2022, a specific input was received from reliable sources regarding presence/hiding of some unknown 
terrorist(s) possessing illegal arms/ammunition in Bishamber Nagar, Konkhan Dalgate locality of Srinagar. Immediately acting 
on the tip-off, local Police/PC Srinagar/Valley QAT CRPF were constituted and the area was jointly cordoned-off for searches 
under the supervision of SSP Rakesh Balwal with overall supervision of ADGP Kashmir, Srinagar to track-down the hiding 
terrorist(s). 


During search of one residential house, the hiding terrorist(s) opened indiscriminate fire upon the CASO parties with 
the intention to kill besides hurled grenades with the aim to manage their escape. The fire was retaliated by the searching parties 
effectively without caring for their personal lives and in the exchange of fire between the two sides, resulting into injuries to 
06 personnel including 02 CRPF personnel viz; Const/GD Aditya and Const/GD Raman Kumar and 04 personnel of PC 
Srinagar viz; DySP Mohan Lal, ASI Feroz Ahmad, SgCt. Mohd Iqbal, and SgCt. Rouf Ahmad Wani. 


Immediately, an operational plan was jointly chalked out by ADGP Vijay Kumar, SSP Rakesh Balwal and SP Iftkhar 
Talib. According to the plan, 02 teams comprising of SP Iftkhar Talib, SP Zoheb Tanveer, DySP Syed Sleet Shah, ASI Faroz 
Ahmad, HC Mushtaq Ahmad, HC Showkat Hussain, HC Saleem Ahmad, SgCt. Mohd Iqbal, SgCt. Abdul Rouf Wani, SgCt. 
Kuldeep Singh, SgCt. Safiullah Qadri, Const. Irshad Ahmad, Const. Amir Ahmad and Const. Manjeet Singh Chib and Foll. 
Mehraj ud Din Khan was assigned the task of elimination of terrorists by advancing from the front side of target house and 
evacuation and shifting of inmates of adjoining houses to nearby safer place to avoid any collateral damage. 


The 2nd team led by SP Tanushree, DySP Mohan Lal Sharma, DySP Shabir Ahmad Khan, DySP Rohit Kumar, Inspr. 
Tariq Ahmad, HC Gayour-ul-Hag, HC Javid Ahmad, SgCt. Mohd Saleem, SgCt. Abdul Rouf Wani, Const. Ab. Rashid, Const. 
Mohd Owais, Const. Irshad Ahmad and SgCt. Khursheed Ahmad alongwith adequate nafri of Valley QAT CRPF was assigned 
the task to approach the target house from rear side. 


After proper planning, a small team led by DySP Mohan Lal despite sustaining injuries, initiated search operation in 
the target location wherein terrorists were hiding. The police party laid the inner cordon and identified the target house where 
terrorist(s) were hiding and reached closer to the target house to cover up the most possible route which could have taken up 
by terrorists to escape during exchange of fire. This was noticed by hiding terrorists and they lobbed grenades amid 
indiscriminate firing towards the search team and they sustained multiple splinter injuries. 


While displaying incomparable bravery, uncommon courage and without caring for his life DySP Mohan Lal shifted 
his buddies and house inmates from the firing area. Despite this, the officer kept advancing in the volley of fire and displaying 
indomitable courage and exceptional tactical acumen and neutralized one Pakistani terrorist commander in a face off gunfight 
from very closer range, who later identified as Abu Arsalan @ Khalid R/o Pakistan “A-category” of LeT outfit. In the process, 
the 2nd Pakistani mercenary also got injured. 


Soon after elimination of Pakistani terrorist Abu Arsalan @ Khalid, a small team led by DySP Mohan Lal backed by 
other joint operational parties comprising of SP Tanushree, DySP Shabir Ahmad Khan, DySP Rohit Kumar, SgCt Abdul Rouf 
Wani, Foll. Mehraj-ud-din Khan in particular entered the target house. Noticing the movement of these personnel, the 2nd 
hiding Pakistani terrorist opened indiscriminate firing and lobbed grenades towards the said party but they retaliated the fire. 
Amid tremendous grid, valour and courage, second terrorist was also neutralized and later-on identified as “Mohammad Bhai 
@ Abu Qasim @ Mir Sahib Pakistan, “A-category” terrorist of LeT outfit. Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the 
possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No19/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 13, 16, 18, 19 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered 
in P/S Khanyar for further investigation. These terrorists were involved in firing incident upon Naka party of CRPF at Maisuma 
Chowk Srinagar resulting into martyrdom of a CRPF personnel and injuries to other. In this connection, a case FIR No. 05/2022 
U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 Arms Act; 13, 16 ULA(P) Act stands registered at Police Station Maisuma, Srinagar. 


In this operation, S/Shri Mohan Lal, Deputy Superintendent of Police and Abdul Rouf Wani, SgCT of Jammu & 
Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th

No. 56-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Manipur Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Langoljam Lenin Singh Assistant Sub-Inspector GM 

2: Sidrath Thokchom Assistant Sub-Inspector GM 

3. Thounaojam Nichal Singh Havildar GM 

4. Kumam Premananda Singh Rifleman GM 

5: Ngasepam Vim Singh Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Following the communal flare-up erupted between the Meetei community and Kuki community in the state of Manipur 
since 3rd May 2023, the Manipur Police personnel came to light that Kuki militants heavily armed with sophisticated weapons 
resorted to indiscriminate arson of houses, vehicles destroying household properties in many places in and around Moreh 
market, such as Moreh town, Moreh Khunou Leikai and Prem Nagar etc. Immediately, police personnel of Moreh Police Station 
and Special Police Commando personnel were pressed into service hectically to control the situation. 


It is imperative to mention that in the said communal flare-up Chin Kuki militants influxed from Myanmar into the 
territorial boundary of Manipur taking undue advantage of porous territorial boundary Line were indulging in nefarious and 
criminal acts with intent to eliminate the Meetei community, thereby resulting to hoarse-crying of innocent people to save their 
lives and soul. 


In the given situation, the Manipur Police faced acute problems due to shortage of manpower, arms and ammunition, 
etc. to cope with the large-scale criminal acts of heavily armed Kuki militants. However, the Special Commando personnel 
under the able command and leadership of Shri Thokchoin Krishnatombi Singh, Superintendent of Police (Railways), In- 
Charge, Special Commando Unit, Khabeisoi, Manipur with strong determination and commitment to save the lives and 
properties of the helpless innocent people, rushed to all the affected areas and did everything which the situation called for day- 
in and day-out. 


In continuation of the attack by Kuki militants upon the innocent people belonging to Meitei community residing in 
and around Moreh town coupled with its attack on security forces as well as on Manipur police personnel, on 17/01/2024 at 
about 4.30 a.m. Kuki militants attacked on the post of Special Commando personnel stationed at State- Bank of India, Moreh 
Branch, by using RPG and sophisticated weapons. After the blast of high explosive RPG, ASI Langoljam Lenin Singh of 
Special Commando along with Havildar Thounaojam Nichal Singh retaliated against the attackers. The attackers continuously 
fired towards the commando personnel from the southern and northern sides. The blast of high explosive RPG damaged the 
entry gate and caused injury to two of the Special Commando personnel namely ASI Sidrath Thokchom and Constable 
Ngasepam Vim Singh. High explosive bomb and sophisticated weapons along with snipers were continuously fired towards 
the commando personnel. In that, ASI Langoljam Lenin Singh, ASI Sidrath Thokchom, Havildar Thounaojam Nichal Singh 
and Constable Ngashepam Vim retaliated towards the attackers on both the directions of North and South in total disregard of 
their safety and lives, even when ASI Sidrath Thokchom and Constable Ngasepam Vim Singh got injured. After some time, 
the injured officer namely ASI Sidrath Thokchom got weakened due to explosive blast injury and profuse bleeding. 
Immediately both the injured personnel were evacuated and rushed to the hospital for medical treatment. 


Thus, the Special commando personnel mentioned above engaged in fierce encounter with the well-equipped Kuki 
militants at the Commando post stationed at SBI, Moreh Branch, thereby preventing the Kuki militants from intruding into the 
Bank. However, there still continued intermittent firing from the side of the Kuki militants surrounding the post of Special 
Commando personnel stationed at State Bank of India, Moreh branch and as such, the commando personnel faced acute 
problem in coming out of the said post. 


In continuation of the attack and consequent retaliation, after a lull for a short time, again at about 8.30 p.m. of the 
same day, the Kuki militants stormed upon the SBI post with incessant firing and blasting big high explosive bombs. At this 
critical, juncture Rifleman Kumam Premananda Singh played a big role in fighting against the militants. ASI Langoljam Lenin 
Singh along with Rifleman Kumam Premananda Singh was firing from the 2nd floor to the attackers by positioning near the 
windows in retaliation of the fierce attack of Kuki militants. Suddenly, a big high explosive bomb blasted on the roof-top of 
the room. During that blast Rifleman Kumam Premananda Singh was hit by bomb splinters in his abdomen and got grievous 
injury. Immediately, Rifleman Kumam Premananda Singh placed him to a safe zone and informed senior officers of the 
development. Rifleman Kumam Premananda Singh called for reinforcement and managed to evacuate the injured personnel to 
the hospital for medical treatment without delay. 


The firing continued till about 11.30 p.m. of the day. During the course of the encounter, the above personnel of 
Special Commando team stationed at State Bank of India, Moreh branch, took vital role, thereby preventing the well-armed 


266 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec. 1 
Kuki militants from causing many unwanted happenings like (i) entering into the said post and save SBI Moreh Branch, (ii) 
snatching of arms and ammunition, and (ill) eliminating the commando personnel, etc. etc. Had the brave commando personnel, 
a few in numbers, not acted in such a degree of professionalism dedication and sincerity at the cost of their lives and safety, the 
incident might have] resulted in another havoc. 


In this operation, S/Shri Langoljam Lenin Singh, Assistant Sub-Inspector, Sidrath Thokchom, Assistant Sub- 
Inspector, Thounaojam Nichal Singh, Havildar, Kumam Premananda Singh, Rifleman and Ngasepam Vim Singh, Constable 
of Manipur Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 17/01/2024.

30. Atul Bhagwan Madavi Police Constable GM
