Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 21/04/2018, Addl. SP, Pranhita (Aheri) Shri. Raja R. received the startling information from his informant that 
about 90-100 armed Naxaiites belonging to the banned CPI (Maoist) organization had gathered in the jungles of Boriya- 
Kasnasur with the intention to eliminate the police force in that area. 


Hence Addl. SP Raja R. after verifying the information immediately contacted Hari Balaji N., Addl. SP (Ops.), and 
shared this alarming input. The 2 of them then brainstormed over this input and reached a conclusion that this input held crucial 
and precise information and the same needed to be communicated to the Superior Officer immediately. 


Addl. SP Raja R. and Addl. SP (Ops.) Hari Balaji N. promptly communicated this information to the SP, Gadchiroli 
Dr. Abhinav Deshmukh verified this input and an action plan was chalked out. 


As per plan, API Amol Nanasaheb Phadtare was appointed as Incharge of the search party. APSI Wauseon Rajam 
Madavi (Party Commander) alongwith 15 personnel of his squad and APSI Madhukar Pochaya Naitam (Party Commander) 
alongwith 25 personnel of his squad were to carry out the operation under the inchargeship of API Amol Nanasaheb Phadtare. 
On 21/04/2018 at 23.00 hrs, the aforementioned team then left in civil vehicles from Aheri for the Bortya-Kasnasur jungle in 
the Bhamragarh division to carry out Naxal search operation. 


After reaching Tadgaoh, as part of the plan, the above-mentioned team then took personnel of the Tadgaon police 
party and the CRPF (9 Btn.) alongwith them for the search operation. API Amol Nanasaheb Phadtare tasked Tadgaon police 
party and CRPF to perform the crucial task of ‘cut-off party’ in the jungle of Kasnasur. 


On the dawn of 22/04/2018, Party Incharge API Amol Nanasaheb Phadtare divided the C-60 squad into two groups 
and reached Boriya jungle from Kasnasur. At about 07:00 hrs, PC Sandip Ganpat Vasake saw 8-10 armed Naxals who were 
dressed in olive green uniforms crossing the Indravati River from the West to the East. He immediately informed API Amol 
Nanasaheb Phadtare. API Amol Nanasaheb Phadtare alerted the 15 Party’s Commander APSI Madhukar Pochaya Naitam and 
also instructed the 2nd group Commander APSI Wauseon Rajam Madavi to turn back. 


APSI Wauseon Rajam Madavi decided to circle back half a kilometer and to cross the Indravati River from the East 
side. He thereafter, instructed APSI Madhukar Pochaya Naitam to cross the river from the West side. The Wasudeo Madavi 
Squad was divided into 3 sections. The lst section consisted of Wasudeo Madavi, 2nd section consisted of PC Narendra 
Dashrath Madavi, 3rd section consisted of NPC Mohan Lachhu Usendi. 


Madhukar Naitam Squad was divided into 4 sections. 18 section consisted of Madhukar Naitam, 2nd section consisted 
of NPC Vishwnath Sanyasi Sadmek, 3rd section consisted of NPC Santosh Vasantrao Naitam and 4th section consisted of PC 
Raju Masa Pusali. The Wasudeo Madavi Party crossed Indravati river while API Amol Phadtare alongwith Madhukar Naitam 
Party reached Indravati river from the West side. Party Commander Madhukar Naitam led Ist and 2nd section and Party 
Incharge Amol Phadtare led 3rd and 4th section. Approx at 9:00 hrs, Madhukar Naitam Party approached the Naxals but the 
Naxals opened indiscriminate gunfire on their party and before they could respond they realized that the Ist and 2nd section of 
Madhukar Naitam Party had walked into a Naxal ambush. Showing calmness of nerves even while under fire, the personnel 
took cover while 2nd section commander NPC Vishwnath Sadmek took his personnel and started steadily advancing towards 
the Naxals. During this gun battle Vishwnath Sadmek got injured while diving for cover. While this was unfolding, API 
Phadtare noticed that the Wasudeo Madavi Party which was on the other bank of the river was going to come under attack by 
Naxals. API Phadtare alongwith 6 personnel of 3rd section jumped into the river to secure them but they too came under heavy 
Naxal gunfire. The river provided no cover from the Naxal gunfire to this team but they continued onwards. During this daring 
river crossing, PC Anil Raghupati Alam and NPC Vilas Maroti Portet got injured. This gave the Naxals the impression that 
they were getting the upper hand in this battle but API Phadtare instructed NPC Anandrao Bajirao Usendi to lob UBGL 
grenades on Naxals. His deadly accuracy with the UBGL made the naxals flee from their places of safety. Party Commander 
Wasudeo Madavi and his squad instantly reacted to the sound of gunfire and crossed over the East to the South side of the river. 
Party Commander Wasudeo Madavi was shocked to see 40-50 Naxals firing indiscriminately on the Madhukar Naitam Party 
and shouting out their deadly intention to each other such as ‘kill the police, snatch their arms & ammunition’. Before Party 
Commander Wasudeo Madavi and his party could comprehend the scenario, they too came under heavy gunfire from 50-60 
Naxals hiding in the forest which lined the river bank. The Naxals then sent 20-25 of their men to advance on Wasudeo Madavi 
and his party to land the death blow. But their deadly aspirations were blown to smithereens when Wasudeo Madavi and 4 of 
his personnel repelled their attack. PC Arun Kailas Meshram was injured in this counter attack. Wasudeo Madavi and 4 of his 
personnel received cover fire and support from other members of his party namely Section Commander Narendra Madavi who 
instructed Vishwnath Madavi to lob an under-barrel grenade on the Naxais. His accuracy with the UBGL grenades made the 
Naxals run helter-skelter. PC Mukesh Kamble was injured in this counter attack. A simultaneous rescue operation was lauched 


268 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec.1 


by Section Commander Mohan Usendi when he sent 4 personnel of his section to rescue 3 trapped personnel. Mohan Usendi 
instructed PC Rupesh Ramesh Kodape to lob a under barrel grenade which he did with accuracy and this in turn made the 
Naxals flee. 


After the exchange of fire stopped, the police team carried out search operation in the area. During the search, they 
found 09 men and 07 women Naxals dressed in olive green uniforms lying injured. They had bullet injuries. They were 
transported for treatment to the nearest hospital and thereafter to Govt. Geneal Hospital, Gadchiroli. In the ongoing, search 
operation the police also found 18 dead bodies of male and female Naxals who were dressed in olive greeri uniforms. Apart 
from the 34 Naxal bodies, the police team recovered Three AK 47 Rifles, Two SLR Rifles, One Carbine Rifles, One INSAS 
Rifle with Magazine, Three .303 Rifles, Three 8 MM Rilfes, Eleven 12 Bore Rifles, Four Magazine of AK Rifle, One Magazine 
of SLR, Two Magazine of .303 Rifles and Ammunitions. Also, a number of medical kits, literatures of banned CPI (M) 
organization and other items belonging to the Naxal were recovered. 


The said encounter can only be termed as HISTORIC in every sense of the word, be it receipt of confidential 
information, perfect planning and precise execution. This encounter tested and proved beyond doubt the bravery and resolve 
of the Officer and C-60 Commandos. The two police teams led by API Amol Nanasaheb Phadtare, Party Commanders APSI 
Wauseon Rajam Madavi and APSI Madhukar Pochaya Naitam faced an unprecedented scenario on 2 fronts, namely, the large 
number of armed Naxals hellbent on violence and a terrain which was made up of a river and river banks which provided little 
or no cover to the teams. Their exemplary courage is proved beyond doubt when they went head-on into the massive volley of 
Naxal gunfire knowing well that they have little or no cover to protect themselves. The police teams faced simultaneous Naxal 
gunfire from 4-5 fronts and though being outnumbered by 2:1, yet the commandos showed remarkable calmness of nerves 
when they replied with precise and controlled return fire. The true test of the C-60 commando resolve came when their fellow 
commandos were trapped and faced eminent death. The commandos paid no heed to the safety of their own lives and rushed 
to the aid of their trapped fellow commandos and successfully brought them to safety. 


The police Officer and members of the C-60 Commando team were able to rescue the trapped policemen and quell 
the Naxal attack. They achieved all this without losing even a single life of any police personnel. 


The encounter lasted for nearly three hours in which 34 Anti-National Naxals including two top leaders and two Dalam 
Commanders of South Gadchiroli Area were neutralized. 


In this operation, S/Shri Amol Nanasaheb Phadtare, Assistant Police Inspector, Wasudeo Rajam Madavi, Assistant 
Police Sub-Inspector, Madhukar Pochayya Naitam, Assistant Police Sub-Inspector, Santosh Vasantrao Naitam, Naik Police 
Constable, Late Sudhakar Bitaji Veladi, Naik Police Constable, Vilas Maroti Portet, Naik Police Constable, Vishwnath Sanyasi 
Sadmek, Naik Police Constable, Dnyaneshwar Sadashiv Tore, Naik Police Constable, Dilip Wasudeo Sadmek, Naik Police 
Constable, Ramsu Devu Narote, Naik Police Constable, Anandrao Bajirao Usendi, Naik Police Constable, Raju Pandit 
Chavhan, Naik Police Constable, Arun Kailas Meshram, Police Constable, Nitesh Gangaram Veladi, Police Constable, Mohan 
Lachhu Usendi, Police Constable, Sandip Ganpat Vasake, Police Constable, Kailas Devu Kowase, Police Constable, Haridas 
Maharu Kulyeti, Police Constable, Kishor Chanti Talande, Police Constable, Anil Raghupati Alam, Police Constable, Narendra 
Dashrath Madavi, Police Constable, Akash Ashok Uikey, Police Constable, Late Kare Irpa Atram, Police Constable, Raju Masa 
Pusali, Police Constable, Mahesh Dattuji Jakewar, Police Constable, Rupesh Ramesh Kodape, Police Constable, Mukesh 
Shankar Sadmek, Police Constable, Yogendrarao Upendrarao Sadmek, Police Constable, Ghissu Vanja Atram, Police 
Constable, Atul Bhagwan Madavi, Police Constable and Vishvnath Laxman Madavi, Police Constable of Maharashtra Police 
displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 22/04/2018. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 09.05.2023, Shri Atul Krishna Das, DySP, Odisha Police received reliable intelligence input about presence / 
camping of a group of armed CPI (Maoist) extremists in the thick forest area at the outskirt of village Taparang under M. 
Rampur PS of Kaiahandi district. Kalahandi district being a heavily Maoist infested area, Shri Atul Krishna Das, DySP and 3 
members of his Intelligence unit namely ASI Purna Chandra Padhan, Constable Ghanashyam Sahoo and Constable Thabir 
Majhi immediately proceeded to the area with weapons in concealment to collect further intelligence. On reaching village 
Taparang, they noticed some suspicious movement in the area. Suddenly, firing came from the side where the extremists were 
camping and one of the bullets fired by the Maoists hit on the right-side foot of Shri Atul Krishna Das, DySP. Shri Atul Krishna 
Das, DySP and his team took position and cover. Maoists continued firing on the above Police personnel from a house at the 
outskirt of the village. By taking care and caution, this four-member Police team retaliated the fire without caring for their lives 
and showed highest level of bravery. In this exchange of fire, 3 hardcore extremists of CPI (Maoist) including one Divisional 
Committee Member (DCM), one Area Committee Member (ACM) and one-party Member (PM) of outlawed CPI (Maoist) 
were neutralized. Simultaneously, they informed SOG, (Special Operations Group, the specialized anti - naxal Force of 
Odisha). SOG arrived at the spot and searched the area during which one AK 47 rifle with -13 rounds of live ammunition, one 
auto pistol with 2 rounds of ammunition and one country made pistol, Maoist posters, Maoist literature, Maoist dress, knife 
with silver handle, umbrella, torch light, search light, medicine kits, detonator, battery wire and walkie-talkie were recovered. 
In this connection, M. Rampur PS case No. 104, dated 09.05.2023 u/s 147/ 148 / 120-B / 121/ 121-A/ 122 / 123 / 124-A / 307 
/ 149 IPC, r/w Section 25 / 27 Arms Act, r/w Section 17 of Cr. L.A Act, 16 (1) (0) / 18 /20 UAP Act has been registered. 


In this operation, S/Shri Atul Krishna Das, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Purna Chandra Padhan, Assistant Sub- 
Inspector, Ghanashyam Sahoo, Constable and Thabir Majhi, Constable of Odisha Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 09/05/2023.

Deputy Secretary

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


This is a case of “Arms Act’. On specific information provided by the HC Marri Venkat Reddy on 01.02.2025 at 
about 19:00 hours, based on credible information that Batthula Prabhakar @ Rahul Reddy @ Bayyapur Reddy @ Bittu @ 
Sarveshwar Reddy @ Raju S/o Kittayya @ Krishnaiah age -30 years, Caste- Waddera R/o Flat No.A-205, Vajara Jasmine, 
Nanakramguda. N/o Waddepally Area, Irikipenta village, Somala Mandal, Chittor District, who was involved in around 125 
criminal cases, was present at Prism Pub, Gowlidoddi. HC Marri Venkat Reddy along with two head constables proceeded to 
the location at 19:20 hours. Upon arrival, they spotted Batthula Prabhakar, identified themselves as police officers, and ordered 
him to surrender. In an attempt to resist arrest, the accused drew a gun, aimed at them and fired two rounds with the intent to 
kill and escape. While they attempted to overcome him, one of the bullets struck HC Marri Venkat Reddy on left leg thumb, 
causing a bleeding injury. Despite this, HC Marri Venkat Reddy and his accompanies head constables overpowered and 
apprehended the accused on the spot. HC Marri Venkat Reddy exhibited exemplary brave action in fighting back the offenders 
by risking his life. In which a case was registered under Sections 109(1), 121(2) BNS, and Sections 25(1B)(a) & 27(2) of the 
Arms Act, 19590f PS Gachibowli, Cyberabad. Batthula Prabhakar was absconded from in front of Central Prison escort on 
23.03.2022 in which case was registered in Cr. No 148/2022 under Section 224 IPC of PS Arilova, Visakapatnam, 


Apart from that, arrested offender Batlhula Prabhakar is very dangerous, there are more 100 and above offences the 
accused has committed by threatening the public by showing of lethal weapons. He committed several heinous offences attack 
on police, HC Marri Venkat Reddy exhibited exemplary brave action in fighting back the offenders by risking his life. 


In this operation, Shri Marri Venkat Reddy, Head Constable of Telangana Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023.

No. 60-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Uttar Pradesh Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Santosh Kumar Inspector GM 

2: Saurabh Mishra Sub-Inspector GM 

3. Kavindra Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


A broad daylight sensational dacoity was committed on 28.08.2024 at the jewelry shop in district Sultanpur in which 
ornaments and cash worth crores of rupees were looted on gun point. The case was worked out and some accused persons were 
arrested but other accused persons including mastermind Anuj Pratap Singh were at large. A reward of Rs. 1,00,000/- was 
announced on each accused person by the ADQ of Police, Lucknow Zone. Anuj Pratap Singh was a dreaded professional 
robber. In the year 2023, he had committed broad daylight bank dacoity in the Bank of Maharashtra, Surat (Gujrat). 


STF team led by DySP Pramesh Kumar Shukla was collecting intelligence in various districts. On 22.09.2024, an 
input was received that Anuj Pratap Singh, mastermind of this dacoity, equipped with lethal weapons had come to Unnao with 
one accomplice to take back looted property hidden there and was staying somewhere in the Katri area of Koluhagara, PS 
Achalganj, district Unnao. They were about to leave the same night on a motorcycle with looted property to dispose it of. DySP 
Pramesh rushed to Unnao and reached underpass from Achalganj to Kaluhagara on Raebareli-Unnao highway at about 10:45pm 
with team mates Inspector Santosh Kumar, SI Saurabh Mishra and HC Kavindra and others. Two teams were constituted under 
the leadership of DySP Pramesh and Inspector Santosh. The team headed by Inspector Santosh Kumar took position on 
Kharanja road in the east of Achalganj to Kaluhagara while the team headed by DySP Pramesh started checking on an unpaved 
way in the west of Achalganj to Kaluhagara. At about 03.45 am on 23.09.2024, a motorcycle was sighted coming from 
Koluhagara side. On coming near, the person riding the motorcycle was identified as Anuj, a rewardee of Rs. 1,00,000/-. The 
second team attempted to intercept the motorcycle. Meanwhile DySP Pramesh with his team stood in the middle of the road 
and challenged the motorcyclists to stop. The criminals did not care for the warnings and turned their motorcycle on the unpaved 
road to flee but it slipped and fell. On being challenged again Anuj, hurling abuses, started to flee on Achalganj to Kaluhagara 
road by firing while his accomplice ran towards the fields by firing, bending, turning and ducking. Finding no other option to 
overpower the criminals firing indiscriminately, Inspector Santosh Kumar, SI Saurabh Mishra and HC Kavindra, etc. using 
their field skills and expertise and without caring for their lives, displayed exemplary courage, bravery and entered in firing 
range of criminals and fired in self-defence in a controlled manner which hit Anuj Pratap Singh and he fell down injured. When 
the firing was stopped, the team approached the criminal who was lying seriously injured. He was immediately sent to the 
hospital to save his life but succumbed. 


In this operation, S/Shri Santosh Kumar, Inspector, Saurabh Mishra, Sub-Inspector and Kavindra, Head Constable of 
Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 23/09/2024. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary

1. Vinod Kumar Singh Additional Superintendent of Police | GM 

Di Ameet Inspector 15 Bar to GM

4. Manoj Kumar Singh Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Manish Singh of district Varanasi was a dreaded criminal and interstate sharp shooter with a long criminal history of 
about 3 dozen heinous offences like dacoity, robbery, murder, extortion etc., operating in districts Varanasi, Azamgarh, 


PART I—Sec.1] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) 271 


Jaunpur, Ghazipur, Mirazapur. In the year 2021, he brutally murdered ND Tiwari, Correspondent Dainik Jagran and colonizer 
in Varanasi, Jeevnandi, GM Steel Company in Mirzapur. In the year 2022, he committed a daylight sensational murder of 
Samay Singh Chauhan in the Meera Bhayandar Police Commissionerate area of Maharashtra. He had become a synonym of 
terror in Purvanchal. In view of his criminal activities, a bounty of Rs. 2,00,000- was announced by the Director General of 
Police, Uttar Pradesh. 


The STF team led by Addl. SP Vinod Kumar Singh of Varanasi unit was collecting intelligence against dreaded 
criminals and other proclaimed offenders. While collecting intelligence near Bankat Railway Crossing underpass ring road, an 
input was received on 21.03.2022 that Manish Singh Sonu, wanted in ND Tiwari murder case, Chunar General Manager Case 
and Chaukaghat double murder case is going from Harahua to Jansa —via- underpass -via- village Bharaon to commit some 
sensational crime. 


Acting on the input, Addl. SP Vinod Kumar Singh with team mates took position at strategic point in the west of ring 
road underpass, while Inspector Ameet, Head Constable Baijnath Ram and Head Constable Manoj Kumar Singh took position 
on the Eastern side of the underpass and started checking. After some time, two persons were seen coming on a black Pulsar 
motorcycle from the eastern lane. Inspector Ameet attempted to stop the motorcycle but criminals quickly moved towards 
underpass to flee. Inspector Ameet identified the person sitting on pillion seat as dreaded criminal and rewardee Manish. When 
attempted to intercept, they drove their motorcycle very fast to the Western Lane and started running to Harahua. STF team 
chased them in Bolero. After going about 200 meters from the underpass, STF team reached very close to the criminals. 
Suddenly criminals motorcycle stumbled and pillion rider jumped from the motorcycle holding a carbine in his hand. STF team 
got down from their vehicle and challenged the criminals to surrender but ignoring the warnings they opened fire with intention 
to kill. Efforts were made to cordon and arrest the criminals but they kept on firing indiscriminately. Among the bullets fired 
by Criminal Manish, one bullet hit Addl. SP Vinod Kumar Singh and one bullet hit Inspector Ameet from the front and they 
could be saved only due to their BP Jackets, otherwise it would have been fatal. Seeing their lives in danger and finding no 
other option to overpower the criminals, Addl SP. Vinod Kumar Singh, Inspector Ameet and Head Constable Baijnath Ram 
and Head Constable Manoj Kumar Singh taking their duty as supreme, displaying indomitable courage, bravery and using 
professional skills and STF expertise entered in the firing range of criminals and fired in self-defence in a controlled manner 
to nab the criminal. As a result of retaliatory firing, the criminal fell down injured at about 1400 hrs and he was identified as 
Manish @ Sonu a rewardee of Rs 2,00,000-. Injured criminal was sent to hospital to save his live where he succumbed. 


01 factory made carbine Sub-Machine Gun on which number 101 was engraved with magazine, 8 empty and 10 live 
cartridges and one pistol 7.65 bore with magazine and 04 live cartridges were recovered. 


In this operation, S/Shri Vinod Kumar Singh, Additional Superintendent of Police, Ameet, Inspector, Baijnath Ram, 
Head Constable and Manoj Kumar Singh, Head Constable of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage 
and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Gufran, a notorious criminal of Pratapgarh with a criminal history of more than one dozen cases of serious nature like 
murder, robbery and extortion etc.; had become a synonym of terror. In the year 2023, he robbed the iron rod trader and looted 
cash more than one and half lac etc. and Rs. 50,000- from KBS Petrol pump, Sultanpur. On 16.05.2023, he brutally murdered 
his accomplice Tanvir and was threatening the pairokar and witnesses of Tanvir murder case. In view of his increasing criminal 
activities, a bounty of Rs. 1,00,000- was announced by the ADG of Police, Prayagraj Zone for his arrest. He was also carrying 
a reward of Rs 25000- announced by the SP, Sultanpur. 


272 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec. 1 

STF team led by DySP Dharmesh Shahi was collecting intelligence against Gufran by visiting districts Noida, Agra, 
Prayagraj, Pratapgarh, Raibareli etc. On 27.06.2023 at about 03.00 AM, a tipoff was received that Gufran is going to 
Manjhanpur Kaushambi with his accomplice to kill some businessman for extortion. Acting on the input, Dharmesh Shahi with 
Sub-Inspectors Atul Chaturvedi, Pradeep Kumar Singh, Head Const. Sushil Kumar Singh and others reached the Bridge of 
Sasur Khaderi river near Vashishth Sugar Mill, Manjhanpur on Prayagraj — Chitrakoot road. Constituting two teams, DSP Shahi 
headed the first while the second team was headed by Inspr. Raghvendra. The first striking team took position at Sasur Khaderi 
River Bridge towards Manjhanpur and second team took position on main road near Samda Petrol pump and waited. At about 
05.00 AM morning, two persons were sighted coming on a blue Apache motorcycle from Prayagraj side. The pillion rider was 
confirmed as dreaded Gufran. Sensing the police, the driver sped up his motorcycle and run towards Samda, which was chased 
by Shahi and team by the Govt. vehicle. The criminals were challenged repeatedly but all the warning went in vain. In attempt 
to flee, criminals took a sharp turn and run back towards Prayagraj. Seeing the STF vehicles chasing them, they turned on 
unpaved road next to Aman Chicken Biryani shop but motorcycle went uncontrolled and fell down. On being attempted to 
intercept, the person hanging a bag on his back, took out the pistol tucked in his waist and opened fires at police, from which 
Sub-Inspectors Atul Chaturvedi and Pradeep Singh and HC Sushil Kumar Singh had a narrow escape. In spite of repeated 
warnings, the criminal continued to fire. He took out a weapon looking like a carbine from the bag hanging on his back and 
started firing, while his companion ran towards village Bichhaura. In view of indiscriminate firing by the criminal and seeing 
the lives of police personnel in danger and in spite of being in the firing range of criminal, Sub-Inspectors Atul Chaturvedi and 
Pradeep Singh and HC Sushil Kumar Singh, in passion to duty displaying indomitable courage, bravery and putting their lives 
in danger moved forward through slope in the side of road, firing in a controlled manner in self-defence. As a result of retaliatory 
firing, the criminal fell down injured. When the firing was stopped team approached the criminal and found him lying 
unconscious. He was immediately sent to the hospital to save his life where he succumbed. The deceased criminal was identified 
as dreaded criminal Gufran, a rewardee of Rs. 1,25,000-. 


One carbine, 9 MM with 01 empty and 10 live cartridges, pistol 7.65 bore with 04 empty and 04 live cartridges and 
Apache motorcycle was recovered from the spot. 


In this operation, S/Shri Atul Chaturvedi, Sub-Inspector, Pradeep Kumar Singh, Sub-Inspector and Sushil Kumar 
Singh, Head Constable of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Dreaded criminal Pankaj Yadav of district Mau had been a notorious contract killer and a synonym of fear and terror 
in Uttar Pradesh and Bihar for the last two decades. With more than three dozen cases of murder, robbery, extortion and other 
heinous crimes, he carried a bounty of Rs. 1,00,000- announced by the ADG of Police, Varanasi Zone. 


The STF was receiving intelligence regarding Pankaj’s Plan to commit sensational crimes by targeting leading 
businessmen and witnesses and pairokars of the cases. In 2010, he murdered Ram Singh Maurya, an eyewitness in the Mau 
massacre case along with several policemen, spreading fear across the region. 


The STF team led by DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi during the course of intelligence collection visited various 
districts. On 07.08.2024, while collecting intelligence in Gautambuddha Nagar, DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi got a tip-off 
that dreaded Pankaj is going from Mathura to Agra to commit some sensational crime in late night. DySP Dharmesh Kumar 
Shahi immediately sharing the input with Addl. SP Rakesh of STF Agra and calling him towards Mathura, rushed with team 
towards Mathura. 


PART I—Sec. 1] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) 273 

Two persons on an unnumbered motorcycle were spotted on NH 19 from Mathura to Agra. The pillion rider was 
identified as Pankaj, a wanted criminal. DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi pursued them, while Addl. SP Rakesh set up a blockade 
near village Rosu about 500 meters ahead from Braj Ki Rasoi. Despite being challenged to surrender, Pankaj took out weapons 
and fired at DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi who narrowly escaped. When Addl. SP Rakesh with his team also attempted to stop 
them, the criminals turned to an unpaved road, causing their motorcycle to stumble and fall. STF personnel quickly moved to 
arrest them, but the motorcycle driver took cover behind a boundary and opened fire. DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi and Addl. 
SP Rakesh narrowly escaped, while Pankaj started indiscriminate firing from which Inspector Satya Prakash Singh, SI 
Yashwant Singh, Head Constable Randhir Singh and Const. Amit Tripathi also had a narrow escape. Bullet fired by the criminal 
whizzed past next to them. Seeing their lives and the lives of passersby in danger and to arrest the criminals, Addl. SP Rakesh, 
DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, Inspector Satya Prakash Singh, Sub-Inspector Yashwant Singh, Head Constable Randhir Singh 
and Constable Amit Tripathi displaying exemplary courage and bravery came out of the cover and did retaliatory firing in 
controlled manner and the firing from the criminal was stopped. Suddenly, the criminal hiding behind the boundary wall started 
to flee towards the fields, firing at police. STF team quickly moved to arrest the injured criminal. The criminal lying injured 
suddenly got up and again fired from which Addl. SP Rakesh, DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, Inspector Satya Prakash Singh 
could be saved by crawling. STF team showing exemplary courage and valour fired in controlled manner and the firing was 
stopped. Team approached the injured criminal who was immediately sent to hospital to save his life, where he was declared 
dead. The deceased criminal was identified as Pankaj. 


One factory made revolver .32 bore, one CMP .32 bore with live, missed, empty cartridges and Splendor Plus with 
red and white strips was recovered. 


Addl. SP Rakesh, DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, Inspector Satya Prakash Singh, Sub-Inspector Yashwant Singh have 
displayed exemplary courage and bravery and a sense of devotion to duty in most unmistakable terms, and have taken calculated 
risks in fierce face-to- face encounter. 


In this operation, S/Shri Rakesh, Additional Superintendent of Police, Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, Deputy Superintendent 
of Police, Satya Prakash Singh, Inspector and Yaswant Singh, Sub-Inspector of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous 
gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Shri Rajanish Kumar Upadhayay, Dy. Superintendent of Police/Police Circle Officer-City-1 & Crime, District 
Saharanpur, U.P., while conducting late night meeting with Shri Zarrar Husain Sub-Inspector In-charge Intelligence Wing, 
Crime Branch and Station Officer, Police Station Mandi and their police teams, regarding Crime control, detection and 
prevention at Police Out-Post Katehra-I, having received information, through reliable informer, about presence of Criminals 
with heavy firearms &. ammunitionin the mango grove on Saklapur Road to commit some heinous crime. Immediately, they 
sprang into action and heading the accompanying Police Force, quickly moved in their respective official vehicles to besiege 
the criminals. 


DySP Rajanish Kumar Upadhayay reached at Saklapur Road, parted their vehicles clandestinely and moved on foot. 
They reached near Mango-grove and promptly evaluated geography of place, deliberated strategy to besiege Criminals, 
organized three Police Teams, one, headed by SI Zarrar Husain and his accompanying police force, another headed by two 
Sub-Inspectors Incharge of Police Out-Posts and himself, headed team along with S.O., P.S. Mandi and strategically deployed 
Police Teams all around to ensure blockade and arrest of Criminals. 


274 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec.1 


DySP Rajanish Kumar Upadhayay accompanying his team, took position behind a wall and heard whispering voices 
and seeing Criminals sitting with firearms in their hands, he fearlessly moved ahead and loudly challenged them to surrender, 
but seeing sudden presence of police, baffled Criminals, present inside, opened abrupt incessant firing towards police to kill & 
continued it, wherein Police Team escaped and they too resorted control reply-firing. 


DySP Rajanish Kumar Upadhayay during continued incessant firing by crime-clever Criminals, visualizing safety of 
the Police Teams in danger and possible escape of criminals, he again challenged them to surrender and he himself along with 
SI Zarrar Husain, SI Sunil Singh & Constable Kunal Malik and Team, determined to restrain their firing & arrest them. With 
conspicuous courage & gallant, policing-skill, they tactically & boldly moved forward in firing range of criminals and barbarous 
criminals daringly fired, hitting DySP Rajanish Kumar Upadhayay & SI Zarrar Husain in front and causing SI Sunil Singh & 
Constable Kunal Malik injured. 


DySP Rajanish Kumar Upadhayay in this situation of ‘do or die’, he with leadership of highest order, himself, SI 
Zarrar Husain, SI Sunil Singh & Constable Kunal Malik, determined to arrest Criminals alive and without caring for their lives 
& limbs displaying conspicuous gallant, courage & devotion to duty, boldly fought back with indomitable courage & firing in 
self-defence, tactically rallied in face of Criminals, causing one Criminal, trying to fire, to fell down fluttered & injured, who 
later died and identified as Dreaded Robber, Gangster History-Sheeter & Armed Absconder Criminal, having Reward of 
50,000/- Aabad S/o Lakhmira and illicit fire-arms & ammunition were successfully recovered. 


In this operation, S/Shri Rajanish Kumar Upadhyay, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Zarrar Husain, Sub-Inspector, 
Sunil Singh, Sub-Inspector and Kunal Malik, Constable of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and 
devotion to duty of a higher order.

No. 65-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Central Reserve Police Force (CRPF):— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Amoghavarsha US Assistant Commandant GM 

2; Koragokulavishwanathgoud Head Constable GM 

3 P. Binu Head Constable GM 

4. Sabir Kumar Constable GM 

5: Sandeep Singh Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 16 November 2023 at approximately 1430 hours, Commandant 18 CRPF received intelligence inputs from unit 
Int. Cell regarding the presence of terrorists in Village Samnoo, under PS D.H. Pora, District Kulgam, J&K. 34 RR was already 
underway with an anti-terrorist operation. Swiftly corroborating the information with J&K Police and RR, Commandant 18 
CRPF promptly mobilized and deployed troops to the location. 


After reacting the vicinity of target house at approximately 1520 hours, the joint forces of 18 CRPF, 34 RR, and JKP 
laid a cordon surrounding the area. As the cordon was being laid, hidden terrorists opened indiscriminate fire, confirming their 
presence. 


QAT Commander of 18 CRPF, Shri Amoghavarsha US, AC split his team into two groups: one positioned on the 
Western side, 50 meters from the target house near a Nala and the second team, along with 34 RR, took position in the South- 
Eastern direction. After tactical positioning, heavy fire of the terrorists was effectively retaliated by troops. In order to make 
effective counter attack, Shri Amoghavarsha US, AC tactically advanced his team closer to the target house amidst the volley 
of fire to have clear sight of the targeted house. This positioning was critical for strengthening the cordon around the target 
house and preventing any potential escape of the terrorists. As the firing continued from both sides till the onset of darkness 
being a vast area, it was jointly decided to maintain the cordon intact throughout the night and resume the operation at first 
light. 


PART I—Sec.1] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) 275 


However, during the intervening night of 16-17 November 2023 at approximately 0215 hours, the situation suddenly 
escalated when two terrorists were spotted trying to flee from the targeted house. They opened fire indiscriminately at the 
security forces in a clear attempt to escape. When the fleeing terrorists came in front of troops, team- 01 of QAT-18 CRPF 
under command Shri Amoghavarsha US, AC comprising HC Koragokulavishwanathgoud, CT Sandeep Singh and CT Sabir 
Kumar from Western side, effectively engaged one of the terrorists resulting in his neutralization. The second terrorist made a 
desperate bid for safety, fleeing towards the South-Eastern side, where second part of QAT of 18 CRPF including HC P. Binu 
was already strategically positioned. The troops showing courage and without caring for their lives, neutralized the fleeing 
terrorists by effectively firing upon him. 


Intermittent firing continued throughout the night. In the morning of 17 November, remaining terrorists started heavy 
firing, which was effectively retaliated. The exchange of fire continued till 1000 Hrs from both sides. Thereafter, on the 
cessation of firing from the terrorist’s side, a joint search of the target area was carried out at around 1100 Hrs. During thorough 
search of the target house, bodies of five terrorists were recovered along with their arms and ammunition. They slain terrorists 
were identified as Yasir Bilal Bhat, Hanzallah Yaqoob Shah, Ubaid Ahmad Padder, Danish Hamid Thoker and Sameer Farooq 
Sheikh, members of the Lashkar-e-Taiba (LeT) outfit. 


This successful operation stands as a testament to the exemplary leadership, indomitable courage, conspicuous bravery 
and operational skill of the troops involved. Despite extreme hostility, Amoghavarsha US, Assistant Commandant, 
Koragokulavishwanathgoud, Head Constable, P. Binu, Head Constable, Sabir Kumar, Constable and Sandeep Singh, Constable 
demonstrated exceptional gallantry in eliminating the threat and securing the area. Their fearless actions under fire, unwavering 
dedication and professionalism upheld the highest traditions of the force. 


In this operation, S/Shri Amoghavarsha US, Assistant Commandant, Koragokulavishwanathgoud, Head Constable, P. 
Binu, Head Constable, Sabir Kumar, Constable and Sandeep Singh, Constable of CRPF displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 16/11/2023. 

SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 66-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Central Reserve Police Force (CRPF):— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
1. Shri Nigam Prasad Second-In-Command GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 11th June 2022 at around 1820 hrs, a specific input was received from SSP Pulwama about the presence of 03 
terrorists in Village Drubgam, PS Rajpora, District Pulwama (J&K). Accordingly, a Cordon and Search Operation (CASO) 
was planned by Commandant 182/183 CRPF, CO 44 RR, and JKP. A joint team of 182/183 CRPF, 44 RR and JKP, on reaching 
the target area at about 1830 hrs, cordoned off the suspected houses and sealed all possible escape routes. 


Shri Nigam Prasad, Second-In-Command, along with his QAT arrived at the location at 1850 hrs and reinforced the 
inner cordon. The joint teams positioned themselves strategically around the target house. Shri Nigam Prasad, Second-In- 
Command, covered the Northern side, while Shri Chandan Kumar, AC, covered the Northwest. QAT/182 CRPF positioned 
itself to the West, while teams from 44 RR and JKP secured the Eastern and Southern sides. Outer cordon teams managed law 
and order situations. 


The terrorist’s position was identified and civilians were safely evacuated from the target house. An appeal for 
surrender was made but the same was ignored by the terrorists. As the search began, the terrorists inside the house opened 
indiscriminate fire on the joint troops, which was effectively retaliated. 


During the exchange of fire at around 2015 hrs, one terrorist attempted to escape towards an orchard, using the 
darkness as cover. However, Shri Nigam Prasad, Second-In-Command along with HC Ajay Kumar and CT Heikham 
Priyonanda Singh of 183 Bn CRPF, displaying exceptional courage, without caring for their personal safety, engaged the fleeing 
terrorist and neutralized him. Around the same time, an unruly mob gathered and started pelting stones at the security forces in 
an attempt to disrupt the operation. The situation was effectively controlled by the troops in the outer cordon. 


Through the night, intermittent firing continued. At approximately 0445 hrs, the second terrorist attempted to escape 
towards the North-East side, where troops already positioned, engaged him in a fierce firefight and finally neutralized him. 


276 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec.1 


The third terrorist remained inside, engaging in sporadic gunfire from all sides. Shri Chandan Kumar, AC and his 
buddy, CT Anil Kumar Chark, tactically moved towards the Northern side of the targeted house and took position near a 
window. At 0515 hrs, the terrorist approached the window and opened fire. Shri Chandan Kumar, AC and CT Anil Kumar 
Chark without concern for personal safety, retaliated effectively, thereby neutralizing the terrorist. 


As news of the terrorists' deaths spread, mobs again started pelting stones at the security forces, but troops exercised 
restraint and managed the situation. 


After securing the area, a search operation was conducted, the bodies of three terrorists identified as Fasil Nazir Bhat, 
Irfan Ahmad Malik, and Junaid Qadir Sheergujree along with their Arms and Ammunition were recovered from encounter site. 


This operation stands as a testament to the exemplary leadership, conspicuous gallantry and operational skill of the 
troops involved. Despite extreme hostility, they demonstrated exceptional bravery in eliminating the threat and securing the 
area. Their fearless actions under fire, unwavering dedication, and professionalism upheld the highest traditions of the CRPF. 


In this operation, Shri Nigam Prasad, Second-In-Command of CRPF displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and 
devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 11/06/2022. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 67-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Central Reserve Police Force (CRPF):— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Joginder Singh Sub-Inspector GM 

2 Rajneesh Kumar Mishra Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 04 January 2024, based on credible intelligence inputs that 02 -03 terrorists including Bilal Ahmad Bhat (a top 
LeT commander) were hiding in Village Chotigam, Distt Shopian (J&K), a joint operation was meticulously planned by 
Commandant 178 CRPF, CO 34 RR and JKP. 


As per the plan, Shri Pramendra Narayan DC along with Unit QAT promptly moved to the location. Simultaneously, 
Shri Teja Ram Choudhary, AC reached the site with joint teams of CRPF, SOG/JKP, and 34 RR. 


After securing the area, a strategic cordon covering 10 structures was established. Shri Pramendra Narayan, DC and 
his buddy Ct Riyajul Haque manned the inner cordon from the South-East with firebase support led by Shri Teja Ram 
Choudhary, AC; SI Joginder Singh and HC Rajneesh Kumar Mishra. 


As per confirmation of the presence of Bilal Ahmad Bhat on the first floor of House No. 89, the inner cordon was 
tightened. Upon being urged to surrender, the terrorist instead attempted to escape, moving into a cowshed and then to a newly 
built structure (T/89), thereby remaining concealed until 0340 hrs. At 0345 hrs, he launched an intense attack on troops of 
QAT-178 CRPF. Despite the heavy fire, QAT responded with precision and resolve. 


Later, the terrorist retreated to House No. 89, engaging in indiscriminate firing. The operation was temporarily paused 
till the first light. In the morning, the terrorist resumed firing from within the house, constantly changing positions. Eventually, 
he descended to the ground floor and remained unseen until 1030 hrs. 


Suddenly, he leapt out from a window, firing in all directions in a desperate attempt to escape. Demonstrating 
exceptional bravery, Shri Pramendra Narayan, DC and Ct/GD Riyajul Haque immediately engaged him with direct fire. 
Simultaneously, Shri Teja Ram Choudhary, AC; SI Joginder Singh, and HC Rajneesh Kumar Mishra at the firebase also 
delivered heavy and accurate fire resulting in the neutralization of the terrorist. 


The neutralized terrorist was identified as Bilal Ahmad Bhat, a top LeT commander responsible for multiple heinous 
crimes including the murder of Army officer Lt. Umer Fayaz (2017), Kashmiri Pandit Sunil Kumar (2022) and others. He was 
also involved in grenade attacks, killings of civilians, and recruitment of over a dozen local youths into terrorism. 


PART I—Sec.1] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) 277 


The courage, professionalism and composure displayed by officers and troops of 178 CRPF especially SI Joginder 
Singh, and HC Rajneesh Kumar Mishra, under extremely hostile conditions were of the highest order. The joint operation not 
only neutralized a top terrorist but also ensured zero civilian casualties, showcasing tactical excellence and gallantry. 


In this operation, S/Shri Joginder Singh, Sub-Inspector and Rajneesh Kumar Mishra, Head Constable of CRPF 
displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 04/01/2024. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 68-Pres/2026—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) and 2nd Bar to GM to the 
under mentioned personnel of Central Reserve Police Force (CRPF):— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

1. Narender Yadav Second-In-Command 2nd Bar to GM 

2. Thakur Diwakar Singh Deputy Commandant GM 

3. Vinay Kumar Assistant Commandant 2nd Bar to GM 

4. Bua Ditta Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Based on intelligence inputs from SOG Srinagar and SP Fast, Valley QAT along with SOG Srinagar were monitoring 
the movement of Mohammad Bhai @ Mubashir Bhai and Abu Arsalan @ Aadil (both R/o Pakistan and Grading A category 
LeT Foreign terrorists), who were involved in attack of 02 CRPF personnel of 132 Bn on 04 Apr 22. As per intelligence inputs, 
on 10 April 2022; SOG Srinagar and Valley QAT zeroed the exact location of terrorists after interrogating the suspected hotel 
and house owner. Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC, Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC and Shri Vinay Kumar, AC planned the operation 
keeping the aspect of broad daylight and cluster of houses available near target house in Bishamber Nagar U/PS Khanyar, 
Srinagar, J&K in the outskirt of Downtown area of Srinagar. 


Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC briefed and directed all three-teams to approach the target house as per plan. Team 01 in 
civvies comprising of Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC, Shri Vinay Kumar, AC and Ct Bua Ditta firstly evacuated the house owner's 
daughter, wife and one civilian working inside room and approached towards the room on first floor where terrorists were 
staying. Team 02 led by Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC followed team one, secured the evacuated civilians and laid a strong 
cordon near target room to block any escape route and support Team 01 with cover fire. Team 03 was tasked to lay cordon 
around target house for establishing fire bases. 


As per plan, Team 01 led by Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC approached towards room, took position and directed house 
owner to knock the door. One terrorist opened the door but sensing presence of SFs, he fired and tried to pull the house owner 
inside the room. Shri Vinay Kumar, AC jumped over the terrorist, fired on him, injured him fatally. Shri Narender Yadav, 2IC 
and Ct Bua Ditta also fired upon terrorist and dragged the house owner outside the room. In the meantime, Shri Diwakar Singh, 
DC had already positioned near stairs and started giving cover fire to team 01. Another terrorist inside room also fired and 
lobbed grenade upon the teams. Sensing the situation, Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC evacuated the house owner and took him to 
second floor. Shri Vinay Kumar, AC with Ct Bua Ditta joined the team 02 on ground floor. Cornering the terrorists on the first 
floor as Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC took position on second floor while Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC and Shri Vinay Kumar, 
AC with team took position on ground floor. Team 03 meanwhile had laid cordon around target house and established three 
firebases in front of terrorist's room. An appeal for surrender was made to terrorists, but in vain. 


Then, Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC directed Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC to lob grenade inside terrorist’s room. 
Accordingly, Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC advanced near target room and lobbed grenade multiple times. Meanwhile, 
leading from the front, Shri Narender Yadav, 2IC whose team members were injured by terrorist grenades and felt to refill his 
team ammunition stock, he decided to get a tactical refill of ammunitions and grenades through improvised means developed 
on site with available items like wire and bucket on the second floor. 


Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC engaged the terrorists from second floor and Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC as well as 
Shri Vinay Kumar, AC lobbed grenades, terrorists took cover inside bathroom of the room. Even the coordinated fire from 
firebaces did not give desired result. As the encounter was stretching and mob started gathering near the encounter site, there 
was possibility of law-and-order situation. Then, Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC decided to carry out room intervention. Although 


278 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) [PART I—Sec.1 


at this juncture, room intervention was very risky bet with equal chances to suffer loss by team. But Shri Narender Yadav, 2 
IC was firm to implement this. A four-man room intervention team consisting of Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC, Shri Thakur 
Diwakar Singh, DC, Shri Vinay Kumar, AC and Ct Bua Ditta of Valley QAT was formed to accomplish the task. While 
approaching towards target room amid the cover fire from fire bases and grenade lobbying, this four-man team was not aware 
about change of one of the terrorist's positions in opposite room. Suddenly, this terrorist opened fire upon this team in which 
team have a miraculous escape and immediately gave befitting reply and compelled terrorist to rush inside the room. 


Meanwhile, the other terrorist came outside from room fired towards the room intervention party and got shifted in 
opposite room. Without losing seconds, Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC decided simultaneous intervention in both rooms. It was 
decided that Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC will go in right side room while Shri Vinay Kumar AC will give cover fire in ease of 
any eventuality. When Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC tried to enter inside room, the hidden terrorist opened fire and lobbed grenade 
towards him and he had a miraculous escape. Sensing the situation, with the help of cover fire, Shri Narender Yadav, 2 IC 
entered inside room and lobbed a grenade inside the bathroom. The impact of grenade in bathroom compelled terrorist to come 
out and leading to close quarter fierce gunfight and neutralized one terrorist. 


On the other side, Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC with Ct Bua Ditta have to approach left side room to neutralize 
terrorists and Shri Vinay Kumar AC gave cover fire in ease of any eventuality. Amidst firing and grenades, Shri Thakur 
Diwakar Singh, DC and Ct Bua Ditta entered the room. They tactically took cover and in the covering fire of Ct Bua Ditta, Shri 
Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC lobbed one grenade inside the bathroom to flush out the terrorist. With the blast impact, the terrorist 
came outside bathroom and tried to fire upon but Shri Thakur Diwakar Singh, DC and Ct Bua Ditta fired upon terrorist and 
neutralized him on the spot. Upon confirmation of neutralization of both the foreign terrorists, a thorough search was made by 
teams of Valley QAT and bodies of both the terrorists were handed over to J8&K Police. 


During the post encounter search, the dead bodies of two terrorists were recovered along with 02 Chinese Pistol, 02 
Pistol magazines, 02 Grenade and other incriminating material. The slain terrorists were later identified as Mohammad Bhai 
@ Mubashir Bhai and Abu Arsalan @ Aadil (both R/o Pakistan and category A of LeT outfit). 


In this operation, S/Shri Narender Yadav, Second-In-Command, Thakur Diwakar Singh, Deputy Commandant, Vinay 
Kumar, Assistant Commandant and Bua Ditta, Constable of CRPF displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to 
duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 10/04/2022. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 69-Pres/2026 - The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Karnataka Fire Service:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
K V Manjunatha Regional Fire Officer GM 

2: Hemanth Kumar D Fireman GM 

3. Mallikarjuna Fireman GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Fire service received a building collapse message from our control room, immediately Regional Fire Officer K V 
Manjunatha, Fireman Hemanth Kumar D & Fireman Mallikarjuna rushed to incident place utilizing available equipment 
rescued 02 labour trapped on the first floor by instructing an excavator operator to position near window, cutting through the 
iron grill & safely evacuating individuals. Being incident commander, K V Manjunatha, RFO interacted with the adjacent 
building residence, he come to know that, under constructing Ground + 06 floors building measuring 40X60 feet collapsed & 
25 labours are working inside are trapped. Immediately, informed to our control room for summoning SDRF & NDRF to 
incident place started rescue operation & rescued 04 labours without injuries. Using rescue items like concrete cutter, victim 
location camera, pneumatic lifting bag, they rescued another 03 labours under the slab. Then NDRF & SDRF reached the 
incident and were placed along with them, they rescued 05 labours & recovered 09 deceased body of labours. The major 
challenge was the narrow roads leading to the accident site which made it difficult for the tender and rescue vehicles to reach 
the spot quickly. The unstable debris & continuous falling of loose concrete slabs created a highly risky environment for rescue 
teams. Due to the presence of iron grills and collapsed reinforcement bars, it was difficult to access the trapped victims without 
specialized cutting equipment. Heavy rainfall during the initial hours of the collapsed structure slowed down the movement of 


PART I—Sec.1] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, MAY 9, 2026 (VAISAKHA 19, 1948) 279 


rescue machinery like crane and excavators. Rescue operation completed on Date 25-10-2024 after worked around 69 hours 
43 minutes. K V Manjunatha, RFO played a crucial role in leading, managing, controlling & supervising the entire rescue 
operation during the building collapse. His timely decision, effective resource management & ensured coordination skill among 
varies emergency response teams functioning of rescue efforts under highly challenging condition, reflecting exceptional 
leadership & commitment public safety and also provided clear direction to avoid confusion & ensure safety. K V Manjunatha, 
RFO, Fireman Hemanth Kumar D & Fireman Mallikarjuna received widespread appreciation for their swift and courageous 
action during the building collapse and also used innovative method like utilizing an excavator bucket to reach victims. 


Honorable Chief Minister of Karnataka, senior officials& the local public lauded the dedication presence of mind and 
tireless efforts of this team. Their professionalism and commitment during such a critical situation reflected the true spirit. 


In this operation, S/Shri K V Manjunatha, Regional Fire Officer, Hemanth Kumar D, Fireman and Mallikarjuna, 
Fireman of Karnataka Fire Service displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 22/10/2024. 

SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary 


No. 70-Pres/2026 - The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Rajasthan Fire Service:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
1. Shri Tara Chand Bhatt Fireman GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Shri Tara Chand Bhatt was recruited as Fireman in the Department of Autonomous Government, Rajasthan on 04 
January 2017. While performing his work with full devotion, honesty and dedication during terrible fire incidents and any other 
disasters, without caring for their own lives, he worked to evacuate the affected people safely in difficult situations and reduced 
the loss of live and property. 


On 31.03.2025 at around 7.34 pm, information was received at the fire station Beawar that poisonous gas is leaking 
in Sunil Trading Company located at Balad Road, Sadon ka Badhiya, due to which the local people are having difficulty in 
breathing. On the instructions of Commissioner Nagar Parisad Beawar, Fireman Tara Chand Bhatt immediately left for the 
spot with two fire fighting vehicles. The fire team reached the spot under his leadership. About 400 meters away from the spot, 
he started feeling difficulty in breathing and burning sensation in the eyes. Considering that nitrogen acid gas is very dangerous 
and poisonous, it was necessary to control the gas leak immediately. As he was the team incharge, it was necessary for him to 
protect his team by preventing gas leakage. Considering that he had very less breathing apparatus, he instructed everyone to tie 
a wet cloth over their mouth. 


He moved forward while spraying water from the fire fighting vehicles. Due to the large amount of gas leakage, the 
lights of his fire fighting vehicles became very dim and a dark saffron colour was spreading all around. 


Due to gas leakage, the company owner and 02 other people were sent to the government Amritkaur Hospital Beawar 
in serious conditions. Along with this, about 50 local people were also sent to the hospital. 


By continuously pouting water on the leaking gas tanker, the effect of the gas started reducing but due to the continuous 
leakage of gas, it was becoming difficult to control the situation. 


About 500 houses around the site of the incident were evacuated and people were sent to safer places. To reduce the 
effect of the gas, he climbed on top of the tanker and started filling water inside the tanker so that the gas started diluting in the 
water and its effect started reducing. 


Risking his life and keeping himself safe, he immediately stopped the gas leakage, thus saving the local people and 
administration from the potential danger. Two people lost their lives in this incident. 


In this operation, Shri Tara Chand Bhatt, Fireman, Rajasthan Fire Service displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage 
and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 14th 
October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 11024/06/2023-PMA dated 16th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 31/03/2025. 


SM SAMI 
Deputy Secretary
